questions_for_human: []
proposed_invariants:
- Analysis renders either its tables or its cards in the DOM, chosen by `useViewport()`,
  and never hides one layout with CSS.
- A figure's pending mark, and its "$" with its number, are rendered only by the shared
  card component (`StatCard`), never by a section's own markup.
- Analysis's month selector uses classes of its own, and a diff of Analysis does not
  edit any `.gastos__*` rule.
- 'Every phone rule that Analysis sets on a shared `gastos__*` element is scoped under
  an Analysis-only ancestor class and the phone breakpoint, and no declaration of
  the base `.gastos__mes*` or `.gastos__mes-boton` rules is edited except moving `order:
  3` under `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes`.'
- A card row renders its label, figure and pending mark from one row object, with
  no spacing or order decided by whitespace in a copy string.
- A sentence that Analysis shows at both widths (the empty-month line and the "no
  best week" line) is rendered by one component, not copied into each layout.
- 'The phone cards render no `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`: their
  cost figures come from `rowCostParts` and `amountParts` through the card''s row
  object.'
- A diff of Analysis deletes none of the bar-label or floating-button entries of Analysis
  (`text-size` on `Customers`, `Home`, `More`, `Sales`, `Varieties`, and the `touch-action`
  and `chrome-*` entries of the bar), and adds none.
- 'Every phone rule Analysis sets on a shared `gastos__*` element is under `.analisis`
  and the phone query, and the only edit to the shared month rules is moving `order:
  3` to `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes` inside the phone query.'
- A card row renders from one `{ before, figure, after, pending }` object, with the
  figure as the only nowrap span and the pending mark on its own line.
- A sentence Analysis shows at both widths is rendered by one component.
- The phone cards render no `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`.
- A diff of this part deletes or adds none of Analysis's bar-label, floating-button,
  `touch-action` bar or `chrome-*` entries.

subtasks:
  layout-branch:
    id: layout-branch
    title: 'Analysis: one layout in the DOM, chosen by useViewport, computer tables
      extracted unchanged'
    brief: "A pure refactor of `web/src/screens/Analisis.tsx`. Phone behaviour does\
      \ not change. The computer capture stays identical.\n\n**Build**\n- Add the\
      \ class `analisis` to the root: `<main className=\"pantalla analisis\">`.\n\
      - In `AnalisisContenido`, call `useViewport()` (`web/src/shell/useViewport.ts`)\
      \ once.\n  - At `'computer'`, render the new `web/src/screens/AnalisisTablas.tsx`.\
      \ It holds today's three table blocks (variety, weeks, months, around lines\
      \ 349–525) and `CostSubRow`, moved without changing any element, class, id or\
      \ attribute.\n  - At `'phone'`, render today's phone markup exactly as it is:\
      \ `.analisis__variedades`, `.analisis__semanas`, `.analisis__meses`, with their\
      \ `RowCostLines`.\n- Shared nodes stay in `AnalisisContenido` and are rendered\
      \ at both widths:\n  - `MonthNav`;\n  - the hero;\n  - the three section heading\
      \ `pie` spans;\n  - the aside.\n- Empty-month line: split the `m.varieties.length\
      \ === 0` ternary so `EMPTY_MONTH_LINE` is one shared node, and only the variety\
      \ list or table sits inside the viewport branch.\n- Extract `NoBestWeekLine`\
      \ (exported from `Analisis.tsx`). It owns the condition (`weeks.length > 0 &&\
      \ no week is best`) and renders `NO_BEST_WEEK_LINE`.\n  - `AnalisisTablas` renders\
      \ it inside `.analisis__tabla-contenedor--semanas`, where it is today.\n  -\
      \ The phone markup renders it after the week blocks.\n- In `web/src/styles/app.css`,\
      \ delete the `display:none` rules that hide one layout per width: around 3053–3055\
      \ for the card containers, the phone hide of `.analisis__tabla-contenedor`,\
      \ and their ≥900 counterparts around 3369–3375. Change no other declaration\
      \ that applies at ≥900 px.\n\n**Tests** (`web/src/screens/Analisis.test.tsx`)\n\
      - Every test sets its viewport through `stubViewport` / `renderWithViewport`\
      \ from `web/src/test-viewport.ts`.\n- Existing table tests (around lines 448\
      \ and 456) run at `'computer'`. Existing phone-block tests run at `'phone'`.\n\
      - Add:\n  - at `'phone'`, `queryByRole('table')` is null;\n  - at `'computer'`,\
      \ the three tables render and no `.analisis__semana`, `.analisis__mes-fila`\
      \ or `.analisis__variedad` is present;\n  - a `flipViewport` test: the month\
      \ selector, the three section headings and the aside are the same DOM node objects\
      \ before and after crossing 900 px;\n  - the no-best-week line shows at both\
      \ widths when no week is best.\n\n**Entry map** (written in the PR description;\
      \ no code)\n- Run the Mobile check (`mobile` project of `npm --prefix web run\
      \ e2e`) on this tree.\n- List every `Analisis` entry in `web/src/mobile-standard/offenders/*.json`.\
      \ For each, name the cell it belongs to: variety card, week card, month card,\
      \ month arrows, hero, since-the-first-record, withdrawal line, aside expense\
      \ breakdown, weekday bars, bar or floating button.\n- Read `scripts/design/mobile_seed.py`\
      \ and record whether the walked Analysis month has, for a variety, a week and\
      \ a month, a nine-digit figure and a pending mark. Record also whether it has\
      \ a current week. Do not edit the seed.\n- If any entry cannot be mapped, or\
      \ the seed lacks a state, escalate.\n\n**Acceptance**\n- Delete any entry this\
      \ diff turns green. None is expected.\n- The `AnalisisCompu` and `AnalisisCompuOscuro`\
      \ captures (`scripts/design/comparisons.mjs`) come out unchanged. Attach before\
      \ and after.\n- The only accepted change to the computer DOM is the removal\
      \ of the hidden card siblings."
    seams:
    - Analisis screen rendered with Testing Library, fetch faked, viewport set via
      test-viewport.ts (stubViewport/renderWithViewport/flipViewport)
    - Mobile check (web/e2e/mobile.spec.ts, mobile project) for the entry map
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    screens:
    - Analisis
    - AnalisisCompu
    branch: agentic/GH-374.layout-branch
    base: b29b0ae8
    reviewed_head: eefd9dbc030384599c476b4116c6ab8d4c3ebfc6
    reason: 'Code and tests hold the brief and invariants 1, 2 and 7. Return it because
      two brief deliverables are missing: the offender entry map with the seed''s

    return_category: spec-scope
  phone-cards:
    id: phone-cards
    title: 'Analysis phone cards: one card component for varieties, weeks and months,
      month selector first, 44 px arrows'
    brief: "Builds on `layout-branch`. Replaces the phone markup it kept with cards.\
      \ Read that subtask's entry map in its PR first.\n\n**Mapping** (`web/src/screens/analisisCards.ts`,\
      \ pure)\n- `varietyCard`, `weekCard`, `monthCard` each take an API row (`web/src/api/analysis.ts`\
      \ types) and return `{ title, headline, rows: { before, figure, after, pending\
      \ }[], current?, mark? }`.\n- Use existing copy keys only. Do not add or reword\
      \ one.\n  - Variety:\n    - title: the name;\n    - headline: `formatMoney(sales_without_shipping)`;\n\
      \    - rows, in order:\n      - share `{figure: share%, after: delMes}`, only\
      \ when `share_percent` is not null;\n      - grams sold with `COMMON_TEXT.soldSuffix`;\n\
      \      - grams gifted with `regalados`;\n      - cost: `before: costo`, with\
      \ its pending text;\n      - margin: `before: margen`, the ` (N%)` in `after`\
      \ only when `percent` is not null, and its pending text.\n  - Week and month:\n\
      \    - title: `formatDateShort(first_day)` for a week, `formatMonthLong(month)`\
      \ for a month;\n    - headline: Sales;\n    - rows, in order:\n      - grams;\n\
      \      - `COST_OF_GOODS_LABEL`;\n      - `SHORTFALL_LABEL`, `PROMOTION_LABEL`\
      \ and `OWN_USE_LABEL`, each only when not null;\n      - `gastos` + expenses;\n\
      \      - `ganancia` + profit, with its pending text;\n      - `caja` + cash.\n\
      \    - `current` is true for the last week. `mark` is `BEST_WEEK_MARK` when\
      \ `best`.\n- Figures and pending texts come from `rowCostParts` / `amountParts`\
      \ (`web/src/screens/analisisCost.ts`) and the existing `pendingSuffix` wording.\n\
      \n**Card** (`web/src/screens/AnalisisTarjeta.tsx`)\n- `role=\"group\"`, named\
      \ by its title.\n- The title truncates with an ellipsis.\n- The headline is\
      \ one nowrap span.\n- Each row is a block with inline content: `before`, the\
      \ figure (the only `white-space: nowrap` span), then `after`. Spaces baked into\
      \ the copy must stay significant.\n- `pending` is its own block line below,\
      \ never truncated.\n- `current` adds a modifier that draws an edge (an inline-start\
      \ border from a token), not only a fill.\n- The week card keeps its `Bar` as\
      \ `aria-hidden`, and `mark` shows the best-week mark.\n\n**Sections** (`web/src/screens/AnalisisTarjetas.tsx`)\n\
      - Maps the three lists to cards and renders `NoBestWeekLine` after the week\
      \ cards.\n- Renders no `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`.\n-\
      \ `Analisis.tsx` renders it at `'phone'`, replacing the old phone markup. Remove\
      \ that markup and any CSS that only it used, and only in phone-only rules.\n\
      \n**CSS** (`web/src/styles/app.css`)\n- Card styles: `analisis__tarjeta*` classes\
      \ under `.analisis`, inside `@media (max-width: 899.98px)`. Never target `.analisis__costos`\
      \ or `AmountRow` classes. Colours, spacing and type come only from tokens. No\
      \ text under 12 px.\n- Month selector first:\n  - delete `order: 3` from the\
      \ base `.gastos__mes` (around line 1282);\n  - add `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes\
      \ { order: 3 }` inside the phone media query;\n  - do not edit line 1511's rule\
      \ or the base `.gastos__mes-boton`. If part 1 of GH-319 has already merged this\
      \ scoping, skip it and say so.\n- Arrows: `.analisis .gastos__mes-boton { min-width:\
      \ 44px; min-height: 44px; touch-action: manipulation; }` inside the phone query.\n\
      \n**Tests**\n- `web/src/screens/analisisCards.test.ts`, unit tests: row order\
      \ per kind; optional rows absent when null (share, percent, shortfall, promotion,\
      \ own use); current week; best mark.\n- `Analisis.test.tsx` at `'phone'`:\n\
      \  - the month selector is the first control (first button in document order\
      \ is Previous month);\n  - each section renders one group per row;\n  - inside\
      \ each group, each figure's text is exactly the formatted value, in the row's\
      \ order, with the existing labels;\n  - each pending text is in a different\
      \ element from its figure;\n  - no table is present.\n- `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts`:\n\
      \  - update the phone locators (around line 294) to the card markup;\n  - add\
      \ a phone test at 320 px and at 246 px, with data built through the front against\
      \ the real backend (as the test around line 183 does): a 60-character variety\
      \ name, prices and grams that give nine-digit figures, and a partly pending\
      \ cost.\n  - It asserts:\n    - `documentElement.scrollWidth <= clientWidth`;\n\
      \    - the name's `scrollWidth > clientWidth` and computed `text-overflow` is\
      \ `ellipsis`;\n    - each pending line's top is greater than its figure's bottom;\n\
      \    - the current week card's computed border differs from its siblings'.\n\
      \n**Offenders**\n- Delete the entries this diff turns green, using the entry\
      \ map:\n  - Analysis's `touch-target` and `touch-action` entries for `button:Next\
      \ month` / `button:Previous month`;\n  - the variety-name `name-overflow` entry;\n\
      \  - the card `figure-split` entries.\n- Do not touch Gastos/GastoEditar/GastoConfirmarBorrado\
      \ arrow entries, bar or chrome entries, or `h-scroll`.\n- The Mobile check passes.\n\
      - `AnalisisCompu` and `AnalisisCompuOscuro` stay unchanged. Attach the captures."
    seams:
    - analisisCards.ts pure functions (varietyCard, weekCard, monthCard)
    - Analisis screen with Testing Library, fetch faked, viewport via test-viewport.ts
    - web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts phone test at 320 and 246 px
    - Mobile check (mobile project)
    depends_on:
    - layout-branch
    status: approved
    screens:
    - Analisis
    - AnalisisOscuro
    branch: agentic/GH-374.phone-cards
    base: eefd9dbc
    reviewed_head: 2a0220b4eef0a208bece65a8bcb324699863fbc8
  hero-aside-fit:
    id: hero-aside-fit
    title: Analysis phone fit for the hero, since-the-first-record and the aside;
      last offender deletions and PR evidence
    brief: "Builds on `phone-cards`. Owns every remaining Analysis content cell on\
      \ the phone.\n\n**Cells this subtask owns**\n- The hero: the `cifra-xl` sales\
      \ figure, the grams/average-margin line and the hero's `WithdrawalLines`.\n\
      - The \"since the first record\" block.\n- The weekday bars.\n- Both expense\
      \ breakdowns, including the purchases line \"Kept apart, added in Stock\".\n\
      \n**Build** (phone-only CSS in the Analysis block of `web/src/styles/app.css`,\
      \ under `.analisis` and `@media (max-width: 899.98px)`)\n- Category names truncate\
      \ with an ellipsis.\n- Every money figure is one unbreakable unit, so `$` is\
      \ never alone on a line.\n- Pending marks are never cut.\n- No text is under\
      \ 12 px.\n- No sideways scroll at 320 px or 246 px with the seed's extreme data.\n\
      - Markup changes in `Analisis.tsx` only where a figure needs its own nowrap\
      \ span. Keep these elements, and their order, the same at both widths:\n  -\
      \ the aside;\n  - the hero;\n  - `AmountRow`, `WithdrawalLines`, `ExpenseBreakdownView`.\n\
      - Change no rule that applies at ≥900 px. `AmountRow` styles may be scoped only\
      \ through `.analisis` in the phone query.\n\n**Offenders** (using the entry\
      \ map from `layout-branch`)\n- Delete every Analysis entry this diff turns green:\n\
      \  - the two `Categoría Larguísimo…` `name-overflow` entries;\n  - the remaining\
      \ hero, aside and since-the-first-record `figure-split` entries;\n  - finally\
      \ `h-scroll`, only once the page passes at 320 px and at 246 px.\n- Delete no\
      \ `text-size` entry: all five are bar labels owned by part 1.\n- Leave the bar\
      \ `touch-action`, `chrome-*` and floating-button entries alone. `aria-busy`\
      \ stays unless this diff turns it green.\n- Add no entry outside docs/product/gate.md's\
      \ rules. Escalate a failure you cannot fix.\n- If part 1 of GH-319 has merged:\n\
      \  - rebase onto it, resolving `app.css` and the offender JSON;\n  - do not\
      \ repeat the `.gastos__mes` scoping;\n  - rerun the Mobile check against the\
      \ merged lists.\n\n**Tests**\n- Extend the phone e2e in `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts`\
      \ with a 60-character expense category name and nine-digit expense figures.\
      \ At 320 px and at 246 px, assert:\n  - the category name ends in an ellipsis\
      \ (`scrollWidth > clientWidth`, `text-overflow: ellipsis`);\n  - the page has\
      \ no sideways scroll.\n- The Mobile check passes on Analysis's walked states.\n\
      \n**PR evidence**\n- The `mobile` e2e result.\n- The offender counts from `scripts/design/compare.sh`\
      \ (`COMPARE_MOBILE=1`).\n- Before and after phone captures of `Analisis` and\
      \ `AnalisisOscuro`.\n- The unchanged `AnalisisCompu` and `AnalisisCompuOscuro`\
      \ captures."
    seams:
    - web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts phone test at 320 and 246 px
    - Mobile check (mobile project)
    - Analisis screen with Testing Library at both viewports (shared aside unchanged)

    - phone-cards
    status: approved
    screens:
    - Analisis
    - AnalisisOscuro
    - AnalisisCompu

invariants:
- Analysis renders either its tables or its cards in the DOM, chosen once by `useViewport()`
  in `AnalisisContenido`; no layout is hidden with CSS, and no test mocks `useViewport`
  or stubs `matchMedia` outside `test-viewport.ts`.
- At computer width the element tree of the three tables, `CostSubRow` and `NoBestWeekLine`
  is unchanged; no CSS declaration that applies at ≥900 px changes, beyond deleting
  the `display:none` rules that hid the cards there.
- 'Every phone rule Analysis adds is under `.analisis` and `@media (max-width: 899.98px)`.
  The only edit to the shared month rules is moving `order: 3` from the base `.gastos__mes`
  to `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes` inside the phone query; line 1511''s `.gastos__mes`
  rule and the base `.gastos__mes-boton` are not edited.'
- 'A phone card row renders from one `{ before, figure, after, pending }` object:
  the figure is the only nowrap span, the pending mark is a separate line below it,
  and only names and period labels get an ellipsis.'
- The phone cards render no `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`; their
  cost figures come from `rowCostParts` and `amountParts`.
- No copy key is added or reworded; card labels use existing keys, in the PRD's order.
- A sentence Analysis shows at both widths is rendered by one component (the empty-month
  line as a shared node, the no-best-week line as `NoBestWeekLine`).
- A diff deletes every Analysis offender entry it turns green, and deletes or adds
  none of Analysis's bar-label `text-size`, bar `touch-action`, floating-button or
  `chrome-*` entries; it adds no entry outside gate.md's rules.
- Routes, `kind` and `parent` in `web/src/routes.ts` do not change, and no cell of
  another screen is edited.

    a spec run and a retry. It changes no code, CSS, offender list, test or routes.ts,
    so it cannot break an invariant or anything the last pass approved.

  scope: subtask
  category: standard-other
  status: open
- subtask: integration
  version: 6
  location: web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts
  claim: The gate's web-e2e row failed on its 1500 s timeout, not on a test. The Analysis
    spec, which this task owns, passes on this head, so I could not reproduce a red
    Analysis check; the full suite runs at merge.
  severity: observation
  scope: outside
  category: process-suite-red
  status: open
- subtask: integration
  version: 7
  location: cf133d19
  claim: The increment is two empty commits (374f3857, cf133d19) that record a delivery
    retry and a cell rerun. They change no code, CSS, offender list, test or routes.ts,
    so they cannot break an invariant or anything the last pass approved.
  severity: observation
  scope: subtask
  category: standard-other
  status: open
- subtask: integration
  version: 7
  location: 'PM: final validation'
  claim: 'On the phone (under 900 px), changing only the viewport height leaves Analysis''s
    rendered content and page height unchanged: no layout swap, no remount, no refetch
    and no loading state, and a Testing Library test proves this.'
  severity: blocking
  scope: plan
  category: spec-behaviour
  status: open
- subtask: integration
  version: 7
  location: 'PM: final validation'
  claim: '`scripts/design/compare.sh --only Analisis,AnalisisOscuro` produces a non-null
    `built` capture for both rows at the integration head, and the capture tool''s
    "page changed when the viewport grew" guard is not loosened, skipped or excluded
    for these rows.'
  severity: blocking
  scope: plan
  category: spec-behaviour
  status: open
- subtask: integration
  version: 7
  location: 'PM: final validation'
  claim: The PR attaches the before and after phone captures of `Analisis` and `AnalisisOscuro`
    from that run, next to the unchanged `AnalisisCompu` and `AnalisisCompuOscuro`
    captures.
  severity: blocking
  scope: plan
  category: spec-behaviour
  status: open
- subtask: integration
  version: 8
  location: web/src/styles/app.css:3265
  claim: '`.analisis.pantalla { min-height: 0; flex: 1 0 auto }` relies on `.disposicion`
    (a flex row with `min-height: 100dvh`) stretching the screen to full height, so
    the bar stays at the bottom of a short page. No capture of a short or empty-month
    page was taken to show this.'
  severity: observation
  scope: subtask
  category: spec-behaviour
  status: open
- subtask: integration
  version: 8
  location: web/src/styles/app.css:48
  claim: 'The Developer reports that the base `.pantalla { min-height: 100dvh }` probably
    moves the shell''s "Nueva venta" button and bar on other tall phone screens when
    only the viewport height grows. This branch rightly leaves it alone (invariant
    9), so it belongs in a follow-up.'
  severity: observation
  scope: outside
  category: standard-other
  status: dismissed

design_items:
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Getting in: More → Analysis (Mas.dc.html, Analisis.dc.html)'
  text: 'On the phone, Analysis is not in the bar. It is reached from More (navigation.md
    line 134, and the prototype''s bar shows More as current). So from another tab
    it takes 2 touches, not the ''1 touch: the tab'' the flow counts, and the end-to-end
    count is 2 + N, not 1 + N.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 1, month selector (Analisis.dc.html)
  text: The drawing does put the month selector first, directly under the title. The
    flow's caveat that the drawing has 'no month selector drawn first' is wrong. The
    drawn arrows are 40×40, under the 44×44 the PRD requires. The written rule governs,
    but the drawing should not be copied as it is.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 1→3, month summary block (Analisis.dc.html)
  text: 'Between the selector and ''By variety'' the prototype draws a month summary:
    ''You sold in the month'' with the headline amount, the grams and margin line,
    Stock shortfall, Promotion, Own use, and the note that the variety table excludes
    shipping. The flow skips this block. It should say where the block sits on the
    phone and that it does not become a card.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 3, variety cards
  text: The flow puts the 'no best week' line in the variety section. It belongs to
    the week section, next to the best-week mark. The empty-month line is the only
    one that replaces the variety cards.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 4, best-week mark (Analisis.dc.html)
  text: 'The prototype shows the best week as one line under the week list (''Best
    week: 05/10 · $ 206.000 · This one: …''), not on a card. The flow puts the mark
    ''on its card'' without saying the summary line still exists or where it goes.
    The flow should say whether that line stays and where.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 4, week cards (sin prototipo)
  text: 'Sin prototipo: the drawing has bars with sales and a cost-of-goods-sold line,
    not week cards with the PRD''s nine figures. The edge or outline on the current
    week, and the pending mark on the line below its figure (the drawing has it inline:
    ''and 1 g without cost''), are judged on the text alone. On the text they are
    coherent. The wording ''The last three of the shortfall, promotion and own-use
    figures'' is garbled and should simply name the three conditional figures.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 5, month cards (sin prototipo)
  text: 'Sin prototipo: the drawing has month bars, not cards. Judged on the text,
    the step follows the PRD.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 6, aside (Analisis.dc.html)
  text: 'The prototype''s order is: weekday bars, ''Your best day is Wednesday.'',
    Expenses of the month (with Flower purchases, ''Kept apart, added in Stock''),
    ''Moved since your first record…'' lines, then Expenses since the first record.
    The flow''s list puts the since-the-first-record lines before both expense breakdowns
    and omits the best-day line. The PRD says the aside keeps its order, so the flow
    should list it in the screen''s actual order.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Variety card, 60-character name
  text: Truncation hides the end of the name and the screen offers no way to see it
    in full. The owner can still recognise most varieties by their prefix, so the
    job is slowed, not stopped. The Varieties screen shows the full name.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Recovery: fetch fails or no connection (sin prototipo)'
  text: 'Sin prototipo: Analisis.dc.html draws no error state. The flow relies on
    the shared ''No connection'' or retry notice, which is coherent with the design
    standards'' notices section.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Step 2, next-month arrow at the current month
  text: 'The flow does not say what the next arrow does when the current month is
    already shown: whether it is disabled or absent, and how that is told apart without
    colour alone.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Analysis phone: month summary headline, extreme data at 246 px'
  text: 'The headline is the shared .cifra-xl (54 px, not 44 px), and a nine-digit
    figure will not fit on one line at 246 px. The design wrongly says there is no
    smaller step: use the existing .cifra-l or .cifra-m for this case and invent no
    size.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Analysis phone: best-week summary line under the week list'
  text: 'The drawing''s line "Best week: 05/10 · $ … · This one: …" is not in the
    product''s copy. Keeping it would add new copy, which the PRD forbids. Keep only
    the card''s existing "Best week" mark and the "None stood out" line; nothing needs
    to go to Tech Lead or PM.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Analysis phone: week and month cards'
  text: 'The card is composed from the variety-card pattern, which is faithful. The
    headline size is written as a range (15–20 px): use the variety card''s 20 px
    so the pattern stays the same.'
- role_slug: ui_lead

# Analysis on the phone: cards instead of tables, month selector first

## Original request

Rebuilds Analysis's phone layout (GH-319 PRD "Analysis on the phone", analysis.md). The computer keeps its tables exactly: `AnalisisCompu`'s capture must come out unchanged. Every new rule sits under the 900 px breakpoint.

What to build (`web/src/screens/Analisis*.tsx` and the Analysis block of `app.css` only):
- **Month selector first.** It is the first control on the phone.
  - Scope `.gastos__mes { order: 3 }` (`app.css`) to Expenses, so it stops leaking into Analysis.
  - If the other part of GH-319 has already merged that scoping, do not repeat it.
- **Cards instead of tables.** Each of the three tables (`Analisis.tsx`, around lines 328, 396 and 459) renders on the phone as a list of cards.
  - Each card shows the same figures, with the same existing labels from the copy module, in the same order as the row it replaces.
  - No new copy.
  - No collapsible sections and no section jumps.
- **Fit.**
  - Headline figures stay on one line, and "$" is never split from its number.
  - No sideways scroll at 320 px and at 246 px (130 %), with the seed's extreme data: a 60-character name and nine-digit figures. Long names truncate with an ellipsis.
  - Every tap target is at least 44x44, the month arrows included.
  - No text under 12 px.
- **The computer** still renders the tables. Same element tree where the two layouts share elements, and only the phone uses the card markup.

Offender lists:
- This part owns every Analysis content cell on the phone. It deletes every `Analisis` entry its diff turns green (`touch-target`, `text-size`, `h-scroll`, `name-overflow`, `figure-split`).
- It does not touch the cells of the bottom bar, the floating "+ New sale" or the bar labels on Analysis. The other part of GH-319 owns those.
- It adds no entry outside docs/product/gate.md's rules, and a failure it cannot fix is escalated.
- If the other part merged first, rebase the offender JSON onto it and rerun the Mobile check against the merged lists.

Tests:
- Testing Library with `fetch` faked, at a phone viewport:
  - the month selector is the first control;
  - each section renders cards carrying every figure of its table rows, in order;
  - at a computer viewport the tables still render.
- The Mobile check passes on Analysis's walked states.

The PR attaches:
- the `mobile` e2e result and the offender counts from `scripts/design/compare.sh`;
- the before and after phone captures of Analysis in both themes;
- the unchanged `AnalisisCompu` capture.

############# Invariants

1. Every phone sheet is rendered by the one shared `Sheet` (web/src/components/Sheet.tsx); no form defines its own backdrop, Close placement, pinned-action layout or scroll lock.
2. A form's element tree is the same on phone and computer, differing only by class and listeners, so a viewport change across 900 px keeps what was typed.
3. A sheet's tap-outside and its Close read the same `touched` value, the `dirty` the host already passes to `useLeaveGuard`; tap-outside never opens a dialog and no form gains a new confirmation.
4. "Keyboard open" is decided only by ADR-0028's `useVisualViewport` through its one pure function; nothing else reads `visualViewport` to decide it.
5. The bottom space for the bar, the floating "+ New sale" and toasts comes from `--alto-cromo-inferior`; no rule hard-codes those heights.
6. A sheet's main action stays inside its own `<form>`, in an actions container pushed to the bottom with `margin-top: auto` and `position: sticky; bottom: 0`; nothing renders below the main action inside the form.
7. A behind-pane is `inert` and `aria-hidden` and renders no chrome, no `h1` and no ids.
8. A diff deletes every offender entry it turns green, keeps registry keys when a form becomes a sheet, never edits a cell of a screen it excludes, and adds no entry outside gate.md's trade or new-row rules.
9. Every new phone rule is scoped under the 900 px breakpoint; ADR-0044's `PriceFrame` dialog branch and `CobroFrame`'s computer branch are not edited, and the computer captures of touched screens are unchanged.
10. Routes, `kind` and `parent` in `web/src/routes.ts` do not change.

Part 2 of 2 of GH-319. The plan and its interview are in `.agentic/tasks/GH-319.md`; the original task branch `agentic/GH-319` is context for reading, nothing to bring in.

## PjM

## Interview: Product Analyst→PM

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1: the job to be done

The `grilling` and `domain-modeling` skills are not available in this environment, so I am running the interview by hand in the same format. This round covers only the job. The product knowledge already fixes most of the behaviour: three tables become cards, month selector first, no new copy, the computer unchanged. Once you settle the job, later rounds cover only behaviour that serves it.

❓ **Q1** - **Job story**: In which situation does the owner open Analysis on the phone, and what progress do they want from it? Today the phone shows the same tables as the computer. I read the tables in the request as the three Analysis sections, but the product knowledge doesn't say what each one holds. The brief only says "When I look at a day, a week or a month, I want sales, profit and cash side by side".
➡️ Recommended job story: "When I sit down with the phone to review how a month went, I want to pick the month first and read each section's figures as cards without sideways scrolling or zooming, so I can tell whether I made money and which varieties and weeks carried it."

❓ **Q2** - **Success criteria**: How will you judge it went well? 
➡️ Recommended criteria:
1. On a phone, the owner opens Analysis, changes month and reads a figure in each of the three sections without any horizontal scroll, at 320 px and at 130 % text size (246 px), with a 60-character variety name and nine-digit figures.
2. Every figure that was in a table row is still there, with the same label and in the same order, and the month selector is the first control on the screen.
3. The computer layout is unchanged: the `AnalisisCompu` capture is identical to the current one.

❓ **Q3** - **What they do today**: What does the owner do now on the phone with the tables? I assume they scroll sideways, zoom, or give up and open the computer. Which is it, and how bad? This tells me which pain to put first in the PRD.
➡️ Recommended: record "scroll sideways and lose the row's name while reading its figures, or wait until they are at the computer", unless you tell me otherwise.

❓ **Q4** - **Priority on the phone**: When the phone cannot fit everything cleanly, what wins? "Phone first" says the working flow ships before polish. For example, if a nine-digit figure and a long name collide on a narrow card, which gives way? 
➡️ Recommended: the figures never truncate or split ("$" stays with its number), and the name truncates with an ellipsis.

### Round 1: answers

**Q1**: Accepted with a rewording. The owner is on the phone with little time (BRIEF, criterion 3), and doesn't set time aside to sit down. Job story: "When I want to know how a month went, from my phone and between other things, I want to pick the month first and read every Analysis figure straight down the screen, without scrolling sideways or zooming, so I can tell whether I made money that month and which varieties earned it." This job comes from the brief's "look at a day, a week or a month" job and applies to Analysis on the phone. The content of the three sections does not change: this work changes only how they are laid out. Which section shows which figure is already defined in analysis.md and the glossary (margin, margin % and average margin per variety and month; best week and best weekday; history since the first record). The PRD takes those from there and does not redefine them.

**Q2**: Accepted. The three criteria are right. I'm adding a fourth, which is the brief's own test for phone work: 4. The owner can read the month's result on the phone without hesitating over any figure. Every headline figure fits on one line, no text is smaller than 12 px, and every tap target, including the month arrows, is at least 44×44.

**Q3**: Accepted as recorded: the owner scrolls sideways, loses the row's name while reading its figures, or puts it off until they're at the computer. A different answer wouldn't change the PRD, because the fix is the same in every case. Assumption: the main pain is scrolling sideways and losing which variety or month a figure belongs to. The PRD leads with that.

**Q4**: Accepted. Figures never truncate, never wrap and never lose their "$". The name gives way and truncates with an ellipsis. A full name stays readable on the computer and on the variety's own page, so nothing is lost. This follows the request and Phone first.

### Round 2: questions

### Round 2: behaviour that serves the job

The job is settled: pick the month first and read every figure straight down the phone screen, with no sideways scroll. I read `Analisis.tsx` and `analysis.md` before asking. Most of this is already defined: the order of figures on a row (analysis.md, "Week by week"), the "and N g without cost" marks, and the best-week and best-day marks told by word. I ask only what they leave open.

❓ **Q1** - **One layout in the page, or both**: Today `Analisis.tsx` renders the phone cards and the computer tables together, and CSS hides one of them. A screen reader or a test would therefore find every figure twice. The request says "only the phone uses the card markup". I read that as: at phone width only the cards are in the page, and at computer width only the tables. A viewport change across 900 px swaps one for the other, and nothing the owner typed is lost because Analysis has no forms. The month selector, the section headings and the aside are the same elements in both.
➡️ Recommendation: yes, one layout at a time, chosen by the 900 px breakpoint. This also removes duplicate figures for assistive technology.

❓ **Q2** - **What leads each card**: Each card shows the same figures in the same order as its row, so nothing is reordered to promote a figure.
- Variety card: name (ellipsis), then sales, share of the month, grams sold, grams gifted, cost of goods sold, margin with its percentage.
- Week and month card: the period label, then sales, grams, cost of goods sold, stock shortfall, promotion, own use, expenses, profit, cash. Stock shortfall, promotion and own use appear only when the period has them, as in analysis.md.
- The headline on each card is the first figure, sales. It stays on one line, with its "$" attached.
➡️ Recommendation: keep exactly that. Each figure carries its existing label, and none is hidden behind a tap, since the request says no collapsible parts.

❓ **Q3** - **A long mark on a narrow card**: "and N g without cost" and a fully pending cost such as "2 g without cost" carry meaning, so they must not be cut off. At 246 px they can be wider than the space next to their figure.
➡️ Recommendation: the figure stays whole on its own line ("$" with its number, never split or shortened), and its pending mark drops to the next line inside the same card. Only names truncate with an ellipsis. This matches your Q4 answer in round 1.

❓ **Q4** - **Marks that must survive on the card**: These are told by word, never by color alone (analysis.md, criterion 4):
- the best-week mark on a week card;
- the current-week emphasis (a differently styled row on the computer);
- the fixed line "no best week" when all 8 weeks are $0;
- the empty-month line when nothing was sold.
➡️ Recommendation: all of them appear on the phone with the words they have today. The current week reads as current by a word or shape, not only by background.

❓ **Q5** - **The rest of Analysis on the phone**: The request names three tables. The aside is not a table: the weekday bars, the since-the-first-record lines and the two expense breakdowns. The request also asks for no sideways scroll and no text under 12 px on every Analysis content cell.
➡️ Recommendation: keep the aside's content and order, and fit it under the same limits (320 px and 246 px, 60-character names, nine-digit figures, tap targets and text size) without turning it into cards. The "Kept apart, added in Stock" purchases line and the long category names must not push the page sideways either.

### Round 2: answers

**Q1**: Accepted. Only one layout is in the page at a time, chosen by the 900 px breakpoint: cards at phone width, tables at computer width. The month selector, the section headings and the aside are the same elements in both layouts. Rendering both and hiding one with CSS would put every figure in front of a screen reader twice. Analysis has no forms, so switching layout across 900 px loses nothing. The `AnalisisCompu` capture must still come out unchanged.

**Q2**: Accepted. Each card shows its row's figures in the row's order, each with its existing label from the copy module. Nothing is reordered, hidden or behind a tap. The name or period label opens the card, and the name truncates with an ellipsis. Sales is the headline figure: it stays on one line, with its "$" attached. Stock shortfall, promotion and own use appear only when the period has them, as analysis.md already says.

**Q3**: Accepted. The figure stays whole on its line. Its pending mark ("and N g without cost", or "N g without cost" when the whole cost is pending) moves to the next line inside the same card, never cut and never truncated. Only names get an ellipsis. That way the owner never reads a partly pending figure as complete.

**Q4**: Accepted, with one limit: no new copy. The best-week mark, "no best week" and the empty-month line appear on the phone with the words they have today. For the current week, the card is set apart by shape, using a word only if the copy module already has one; it is never told by background color alone. Assumption: if no existing word fits, shape alone is enough (for example an edge or outline that colour isn't needed to see). Adding copy would widen this part's scope for a mark the owner already understands from context.

**Q5**: Accepted. The aside keeps its content and its order: weekday bars, the since-the-first-record lines, the two expense breakdowns and the purchases line "Kept apart, added in Stock". It stays as it is rather than becoming cards. It must meet the same limits as the rest of the screen: no sideways scroll at 320 px and at 246 px, long category names truncate with an ellipsis, nine-digit figures stay whole with their "$", nothing under 12 px, and tap targets at least 44×44. The request gives this part every Analysis content cell, and the aside counts.

### Closing

### Closing summary

**Job (settled).** When I want to know how a month went, from my phone and between other things, I want to pick the month first and read every Analysis figure straight down the screen, without scrolling sideways or zooming, so I can tell whether I made money that month and which varieties earned it. Today the owner scrolls sideways, loses which variety or period a figure belongs to, or waits for the computer.

**Success criteria.**
1. At 320 px and at 246 px (130 %), with a 60-character name and nine-digit figures, the owner changes month and reads each section with no sideways scroll.
2. Every figure of every table row appears on its card with the same label and in the same order, and the month selector is the first control.
3. The computer layout is unchanged: the `AnalisisCompu` capture comes out the same.
4. Every headline figure fits on one line, no text is under 12 px, and every tap target, the month arrows included, is at least 44×44.

**Decisions.**
- **One layout at a time.** Cards at phone width, tables at computer width, chosen by the 900 px breakpoint. The month selector, the section headings and the aside are the same elements in both. No figure is read twice by assistive technology.
- **Card content.** Same figures, labels and order as the row each card replaces. Sales is the headline and stays on one line with its "$". Stock shortfall, promotion and own use appear only when the period has them. Nothing is collapsible and no section jumps. The month selector is the first control.
- **Fit.**
  - Names and period labels truncate with an ellipsis.
  - Figures never split or truncate.
  - A pending mark ("and N g without cost", or "N g without cost" when the whole figure is pending) moves to the next line inside the card and is never cut.
- **Marks.** The best-week mark, "no best week" and the empty-month line appear on the phone with their existing words. The current week is set apart by shape, using a word only if the copy module already has one, never by background color alone. No new copy.
- **Aside.** The weekday bars, the since-the-first-record lines, the two expense breakdowns and the purchases line keep their content and order. They meet the same limits as the rest of the screen.

**Out of scope for this PRD.** The content of the sections and the definitions of their figures (analysis.md and the glossary already fix them), the computer's layout, and the bottom bar, the floating "+ New sale" and the bar labels, which belong to the other part of GH-319.

**Open point.** I could not load the `grilling` and `domain-modeling` skills, so I ran the interview by hand. The glossary and the ADRs needed no change.

#### Success criteria

2. Every figure of every table row appears on its card, with the same existing label and in the same order. The month selector is the first control on the screen.
3. The computer layout is unchanged: the `AnalisisCompu` capture comes out the same.
4. Every headline figure fits on one line, no text is under 12 px, and every tap target is at least 44×44, the month arrows included.

#### Scope

- Phone width only, under the 900 px breakpoint.
- The Analysis screen and its styles.
- The content and definitions of the figures do not change.

#### Behaviour

- **Month selector first.**
  - On the phone it is the first control on Analysis.
  - The rule that orders it in Expenses is scoped to Expenses so it no longer affects Analysis. Skip this if the other part of GH-319 already did it.
- **One layout at a time.**
  - At phone width the three sections render as lists of cards, and no table is in the page.
  - At computer width they render as the current tables, and no card is in the page.
  - The month selector, the section headings and the aside are the same elements in both layouts.
  - No figure is exposed twice to assistive technology.
  - Analysis has no forms, so crossing 900 px loses nothing.
- **Variety card** (chosen month): the name, then sales, share of the month, grams sold, grams gifted, cost of goods sold, and margin with its percentage. Share and percentage appear only when they do today.
- **Week card** (last 8) and **month card** (last 12):
  - The period label, then sales, grams, cost of goods sold, stock shortfall, promotion, own use, expenses, profit and cash.
  - Stock shortfall, promotion and own use appear only when the period has them, as analysis.md says.
- **Same figures, same order, same labels.**
  - Each card carries the figures of the row it replaces, in that row's order, with labels already in the copy module.
  - Nothing is reordered, hidden behind a tap or collapsed.
  - There are no section jumps.
- **Headline.** Sales is the headline figure of a card. It stays on one line, and its "$" is never split from its number.
- **Fit.**
  - Names and period labels truncate with an ellipsis.
  - Figures never truncate, wrap or split.
  - A pending mark ("and N g without cost", or "N g without cost" when the whole figure is pending) goes on the line below its figure, inside the same card. It is never cut or truncated, so a partly pending figure is never read as complete.
- **Marks.**
  - The best-week mark, the "no best week" line and the empty-month line appear on the phone with the words they have today.
  - The current week is set apart by shape (for example an edge or an outline), never by background colour alone. It uses a word only if the copy module already has one.
- **Aside.**
  - The aside holds the weekday bars, the since-the-first-record lines, the two expense breakdowns and the purchases line "Kept apart, added in Stock". They keep their content and their order, and they do not become cards.
  - They meet the same limits as the rest of the screen:
    - no sideways scroll at 320 px or 246 px;
    - long category names truncate with an ellipsis;
    - nine-digit figures stay whole, with their "$";
    - no text under 12 px;
    - tap targets at least 44×44.

#### Out of scope

- Any change to what the sections show or how the figures are defined (analysis.md, glossary).
- The computer layout.
- The bottom bar, the floating "+ New sale" and the bar labels on Analysis. The other part of GH-319 owns them.
- New copy.
- Routes and navigation structure.

#### Acceptance

- **Phone viewport, with the network faked:**
  - the month selector is the first control;
  - each section renders cards carrying every figure of its table rows, in order, with the existing labels;
  - no table is in the page.
- **Computer viewport:** the tables render and no card does.
- **Both viewports:** the month selector, the section headings and the aside are the same elements.
- **The seed's extreme data** (a 60-character name, nine-digit figures), at 320 px and at 246 px:
  - no sideways scroll;
  - the name ends in an ellipsis;
  - every figure is whole, with its "$";
  - a pending mark shows whole, on the line below its figure;
  - the current-week card is set apart by something other than background colour.
- **Mobile check:**
  - it passes on Analysis's walked states;
  - the offender entries for Analysis content cells that this change turns green (`touch-target`, `text-size`, `h-scroll`, `name-overflow`, `figure-split`) are deleted;
  - no entry is added outside gate.md's rules, and a failure that cannot be fixed is escalated;
  - if the other part of GH-319 merged first, the offender JSON is rebased onto it and the Mobile check is rerun against the merged lists.
- **The PR attaches:**
  - the `mobile` e2e result and the offender counts from `scripts/design/compare.sh`;
  - the before and after phone captures of Analysis, in both themes;
  - the unchanged `AnalisisCompu` capture.

#### Constraints carried from the request

- Every new phone rule sits under the 900 px breakpoint.
- Only the Analysis screen files and the Analysis block of the styles change.
- Routes, `kind` and `parent` in `web/src/routes.ts` do not change.
- Cells of other screens, and the bar cells on Analysis, are not touched.

### For the next role

For the Tech Lead:

- **Layout.** Only one layout is in the DOM at a time, chosen at 900 px. Do not hide one layout with CSS: that puts every figure in front of a screen reader twice and breaks the "no table / no card" tests.
  - The month selector, the headings and the aside stay as shared elements.
  - The `AnalisisCompu` capture must come out byte-for-byte unchanged.
- **Card figures.**
  - Each card uses the row's existing copy keys, in the row's order. Do not add a key.
  - Pending marks wrap below their figure. Only names and period labels get an ellipsis.
  - Sales is the headline and stays on one line, with "$" attached.
- **Current week.** Set it apart by shape (an edge or an outline). If the copy module has no word for it, add none.
- **Offender lists.** Delete only the Analysis content-cell entries this change turns green. Never touch the bar, the floating "+ New sale" or bar-label cells.
- **Order with part 1.** If part 1 of GH-319 has already merged the `.gastos__mes` scoping or edited the offender lists, rebase onto it and rerun the Mobile check.
- **Definitions.** The updated analysis.md now holds the phone layout rules and their metrics. No ADR is reopened, and the glossary is unchanged.

## UX Designer

_The system denied 1 write attempt: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

### Flow

Only one job story is in the PRD. Analysis has no forms, no confirmations and no new screens. The route, `kind` and `parent` are unchanged, and so is the way in (More, or the Analysis entry in the sidebar or bar). The screens below are the one Analysis tab root at phone width, and the same screen at computer width.

#### Job: "When I want to know how a month went, from my phone, I want to pick the month first and read every figure straight down the screen, so I can tell whether I made money and which varieties earned it."

**Where the person starts:** a tab root, Analysis, on the phone (under 900 px). They arrive from the bar or from More. The screen shows no sideways scroll, and the month selector is the first control.

**Steps and screens (phone, all on Analysis):**

1. Open Analysis. The month selector is the first control, with the previous and next month arrows at least 44×44. The default is the current month. (1 touch: the tab.)
2. Optional: change month with the arrows. The variety section updates. The week and month sections are not tied to the selector. (1 touch per month moved; 0 if the current month is wanted.)
3. Read the variety section as a list of cards, one per variety. Each card has the name, which truncates with an ellipsis, then sales as the headline. After sales come share, g sold, g gifted, cost of goods sold, and margin with its percentage. Share and percentage show only when they do today. A pending mark ("and N g without cost") sits on the line below its figure, inside the card. The "no best week" and empty-month lines keep their current words. (0 touches, only reading down the page.)
4. Keep scrolling down to the week section, the last 8 weeks, as cards. Each has the period label, then sales, grams, cost of goods sold, stock shortfall, promotion, own use, expenses, profit and cash. The last three of the shortfall, promotion and own-use figures appear only when the period has them. The best-week mark is on its card. The current week is set apart by an edge or outline, not by background colour alone. (0 touches.)
5. Keep scrolling to the month section, the last 12 months, as cards with the same figures as a week card. (0 touches.)
6. Keep scrolling to the aside: the weekday bars, the since-the-first-record lines, the two expense breakdowns and the "Kept apart, added in Stock" purchases line. These stay as they are today and do not become cards. Long category names truncate. (0 touches.)

**Where they end up:** still on Analysis, having read the whole screen from top to bottom. Nothing is saved, so nothing can be lost.

**Touch count end to end:**
- Current month: 1 touch (open Analysis), then scrolling only.
- A previous month: 1 + N touches, N being the months back.
- Compared with today, this removes the sideways scrolls on the three tables (at least 3 horizontal gestures per pass, more per row-read).
- There are no section jumps, no taps to expand and no collapsed parts, so the vertical scroll is the only added effort.

**What can go wrong and how they recover:**
- *Fetch fails or no connection.* The shared "No connection" or generic retry notice applies, unchanged. The recovery is the existing retry, and the month selector stays first.
- *Month with no sales.* The empty-month line shows with its current words, in place of the variety cards. The person moves the month with the arrows. The arrows stay at least 44×44 and are not covered by anything.
- *Very long variety name (60 characters).* It truncates with an ellipsis and the figures are untouched. The full name is not visible on this screen. This is a possible friction point for UX Lead: nothing in the PRD lets them see the whole name from the card.
- *Nine-digit figures, or text at 130 % (246 px).* Figures never truncate, wrap or split, and "$" stays with its number. A pending mark wraps below its figure and is never cut. If the headline plus the card padding cannot fit at 246 px, the PRD offers no fallback besides the layout itself. UX Lead should flag it if it does not fit.
- *Partly pending figure.* The mark is always on the line below inside the same card, so the figure is not read as complete.
- *Window resized across 900 px.* Tables and cards swap. The shared elements (selector, headings, aside) stay, so the chosen month is kept. Analysis has no typed input to lose.
- *Screen reader.* Only one layout is in the page, so no figure is read twice.

**Computer (900 px and up): flow unchanged.** Same start, same selector, same three tables and same aside. No card is in the page, and the `AnalisisCompu` capture stays as it is. Touch and click count: 1 to open, plus month changes as before.

**Design caveat for UX Lead, not resolved here.** The drawing `Analisis.dc.html` draws the variety rows as stacked lines of text, with no card for the week or the month and no month selector drawn first. So the current screens do not draw the week or month cards, the pending mark below the figure or the current-week edge. I have not invented screens for these. UX Lead should flag the gap.

### Walk

**Entry screen and path.** In the prototype I went Inicio.dc.html → More (Mas.dc.html) → the "Análisis" row → Analisis.dc.html. The bar on Analisis.dc.html marks More as current, and navigation.md line 134 confirms that on the phone Analysis is reached from More, not from the bar. The flow's "1 touch: the tab" is therefore off by one. From another tab it is 2 touches, plus N months moved. *(observation)*

**Job: pick the month, then read every figure down the screen (phone, Analisis.dc.html at 390 px).**

1. **Open Analysis: month selector first.** The prototype draws the title, then the month pill with ‹ October 2026 ›, as the first control. The flow's caveat that the drawing has "no month selector drawn first" is wrong: it does. The drawn arrows are 40×40, below the PRD's 44×44. The written rule governs, and the flow states it correctly. *(observation)*
2. **Change month.** The arrows in the pill can be taken. The flow does not say what the next arrow does at the current month. *(observation)*
3. **Month summary, then variety.**
   - Under the selector, the prototype draws a month summary block the flow never mentions:
     - "You sold in the month" with the headline amount;
     - the grams and margin line;
     - Stock shortfall, Promotion, Own use, with "and 1 g without cost";
     - the note about shipping.
   - The flow should place this block in the phone order. *(observation)*
   - The variety items are already drawn stacked, like cards: name and amount on one line, the share bar, then share, g sold, g given away, cost, and margin %. This matches the flow's order.
   - The flow puts the "no best week" line here, but it belongs to the week section. *(observation)*
4. **Week section: sin prototipo for the cards.** The drawing has bars with sales and a cost-of-goods-sold sub-line, not cards with nine figures. The pending mark is drawn inline, not on the line below. The current week is drawn by bold label and dark bar, not by an edge.
   - Judged on the text, the flow's cards follow the PRD.
   - The prototype draws the best week as a summary line under the list ("Best week: 05/10 · $ 206.000 · This one: …"). The flow moves the mark "on its card" and says nothing about that line. *(observation)*
   - The step also has a garbled sentence ("The last three of the…"). *(observation)*
5. **Month section: sin prototipo for the cards.** Bars only in the drawing. The text follows the PRD. *(observation)*
6. **Aside.** The prototype's order is:
   1. weekday bars;
   2. "Your best day is Wednesday.";
   3. Expenses of the month, ending with Flower purchases, "Kept apart, added in Stock";
   4. "Moved since your first record…" (Sales … Cash);
   5. Expenses since the first record.

   The flow lists the since-the-first-record lines before both breakdowns and omits the best-day line. Since the PRD says the aside keeps its order, the flow should state the real order. *(observation)*

**Recovery.**
- **No connection or fetch error: sin prototipo** on Analysis. The flow's reuse of the shared notice and retry is coherent with the notices rule.
- **Empty month:** the arrows stay reachable, so there is no dead end.
- **60-character name:** truncation hides the end of the name with no way to see it here. That is friction, not a stop, since the prefix identifies the variety and the Varieties screen shows it in full. *(observation)*
- **Fit at 246 px:** on the drawing's layout (name truncating beside a 20 px headline), a nine-digit "$ 999.999.999" fits beside an ellipsised name, so I see no blocker.

**Computer (AnalisisCompu.dc.html).** Unchanged by this task. The flow's "same as today" is consistent.

No step leaves the owner unable to change the month or to read a section. None of the findings blocks.

### Verdict

`approved`: the phone walk completes the job end to end. The findings are friction or flow text that does not match the prototype: the touch count via More, the omitted month summary and best-day line, the aside order and the placement of the best-week line. UX Designer can tighten them without sending the flow back.
