### Verdict

`approved`. The validated flow and screens design cover the one job end to end on the phone. UX Lead and UI Lead found nothing blocking, so every finding goes to the PR as an observation.

### PRD

#### Problem

On the phone, Analysis shows the same tables as the computer. The owner works from the phone with little time. Today they scroll sideways and lose which variety, week or month a figure belongs to, or they wait until they are at the computer. The three sections are margins by variety for the chosen month, the last 8 weeks and the last 12 months. They and their figures are already defined in analysis.md and the glossary. This work changes only how they are laid out on the phone.

#### Job story

When I want to know how a month went, from my phone and between other things, I want to pick the month first and read every Analysis figure straight down the screen, without scrolling sideways or zooming, so I can tell whether I made money that month and which varieties earned it.

#### Success criteria

1. At 320 px and at 246 px (130 % text), with a 60-character variety name and nine-digit figures, the owner can change month and read each section with no sideways scroll.
2. Every figure of every table row appears on its card, with the same existing label and in the same order. The month selector is the first control on the screen.
3. The computer layout is unchanged: the `AnalisisCompu` capture comes out the same.
4. Every headline figure fits on one line, no text is under 12 px, and every tap target is at least 44×44, the month arrows included.

#### Scope

- Phone width only, under the 900 px breakpoint.
- The Analysis screen files and the Analysis block of `app.css`.
- The content and definitions of the figures do not change.

#### Flow (validated by UX Lead)

The owner starts on Analysis, a tab root, on the phone. They reach it from More: Home → More → Analysis. It has no forms, no confirmations and no new screens, and the route, `kind` and `parent` are unchanged.

1. **Open Analysis.** The month selector is the first control and defaults to the current month. Its arrows are at least 44×44.
2. **Optional: change month** with the arrows, one touch per month. The month summary and the variety section follow the selector. The week and month sections do not.
3. **Read the month summary**, then the variety section as one card per variety.
4. **Scroll down to the week section**: the last 8 weeks, as cards. The best-week mark is on its card. The current week is set apart by shape.
5. **Scroll down to the month section**: the last 12 months, as cards.
6. **Scroll down to the aside**, which stays as it is today.

The owner ends still on Analysis, having read it from top to bottom. Nothing is saved, so nothing can be lost.

**Touches.** From another tab: 2 touches (More, then Analysis), plus 1 per month moved back, then vertical scrolling only. There are no section jumps, no taps to expand and no collapsed parts. The sideways scrolls on all three tables are gone.

**Recovery.**
- **Fetch error or no connection:** the shared notice and retry, with the selector still first.
- **Empty month:** the empty-month line replaces the variety cards, and the arrows stay reachable.
- **Long name:** ellipsis. The full name is on Varieties.
- **Crossing 900 px:** cards and tables swap. The shared elements keep the chosen month.
- **Screen reader:** only one layout is in the page, so no figure is read twice.

**Computer (900 px and up):** the flow is unchanged. Same selector, the three tables and the aside, and no cards.

#### Screens (validated by UI Lead)

**Analysis, phone (under 900 px)**, built from `Analisis.dc.html` with the product's own tokens and components, top to bottom:

1. **Title** "Analysis". The bottom bar and the floating "+ New sale" belong to part 1 of GH-319 and are not touched here.
2. **Month selector**, the first control: the drawn 48 px pill with ‹ month ›. The arrow targets are at least 44×44 (the drawing has 40×40) and the icons stay 18 px. The arrows keep their current accessible names.
3. **Month summary block**, unchanged:
   - "You sold in the month" and its headline;
   - the grams and margin line;
   - Stock shortfall, Promotion and Own use;
   - the note.

   The headline stays on one line with its "$". Where the current size cannot fit at 246 px, an existing smaller figure step is used and no new size is invented.
4. **"By variety"**: one card per variety.
   - Line 1 is the name (17 px, 600, ellipsis) and sales as the headline (20 px, 600, one line, "$" attached, never truncated).
   - Then the decorative share bar.
   - Then label/value pairs (13 px muted label, value at 600) in the row's order: share of the month, g sold, g gifted, cost of goods sold, and margin with its percentage. Margin keeps its meaning by its word, never by colour alone.
   - Share and percentage appear only when they do today.
   - A pending mark ("and N g without cost" / "N g without cost") goes whole on its own line below its figure.
   - Cards are 16 px apart, with no divider.
5. **"Week by week · last 8"**: one card per week, on the variety-card pattern.
   - Line 1 is the period label (ellipsis) and sales as the headline (one line). The decorative sales bar may stay.
   - Then grams, cost of goods sold, stock shortfall, promotion, own use, expenses, profit and cash. Stock shortfall, promotion and own use appear only when the period has them, and pending marks go below their figure.
   - The current week has a 2 px ink edge on the card's start side, with no word. That rule lives only under 900 px.
   - The best-week mark is the existing `mejorSemana` mark on its card.
   - When no week stands out, the existing "None stood out" line sits under the section.
   - No best-week summary line is added under the list, because that would be new copy.
6. **"Month by month · last 12"**: the same card. The range label truncates.
7. **Aside**: not turned into cards. It keeps the drawing's and today's order:
   1. weekday bars and "Your best day is …";
   2. Expenses of the month, with Flower purchases and "Kept apart, added in Stock";
   3. Moved since your first record (Sales … Cash);
   4. Expenses since the first record.

   Rows put the label on the left and the value on the right. Category names truncate, figures stay whole with "$", and pending marks go below their figure. Targets are at least 44 px.

**States.**
- **Filled.**
- **Loading:** the title and selector show at once. The sections show placeholders shaped like cards, not a spinner alone.
- **Empty month:** the empty-month line in place of the variety cards. The week and month sections still show.
- **No best week:** "None stood out".
- **Error / no connection:** the shared notice with the attention icon, below the selector.
- **Pending, partial or whole:** the mark goes below its figure, and a whole pending cost is never shown as $0.
- **Extreme data at 320 and 246 px:** the name ends in an ellipsis, figures are whole, and nothing scrolls sideways.
- **Negative figures:** shown with a minus sign.

The colours are the tokens (`--superficie`, `--tinta`, `--tinta2`), not hex values.

**Analysis, computer (900 px and up)** is `AnalisisCompu.dc.html`, unchanged: the tables, the shared selector, the headings and the aside, with no cards. The capture must be identical.

#### Behaviour

- **Month selector first.** The `.gastos__mes { order: 3 }` rule is scoped to Expenses. Skip this if part 1 already did it.
- **One layout at a time.** Only one layout is in the DOM at a time, chosen at 900 px: cards on the phone, tables on the computer. It is never done by hiding one layout with CSS. The selector, the headings and the aside are the same elements in both.
- **Same figures, same order, same labels**, all from the existing copy module, with no new copy. Nothing is collapsed and there are no section jumps.
- **Fit.**
  - Only names and period labels truncate.
  - Figures never truncate, wrap or split from their "$".
  - Pending marks go whole on the line below their figure.

#### Out of scope

- What the sections show and how the figures are defined.
- The computer layout.
- The bottom bar, the floating "+ New sale" and the bar labels, which belong to part 1 of GH-319.
- New copy.
- Routes and navigation.

#### Acceptance

- **Phone viewport, with `fetch` faked:**
  - the month selector is the first control;
  - each section renders cards carrying every figure of its rows, in order, with the existing labels;
  - no table is in the page.
- **Computer viewport:** the tables render and no card does.
- **Both viewports:** the selector, the headings and the aside are the same elements.
- **Extreme seed data at 320 px and 246 px:**
  - no sideways scroll;
  - the name ends in an ellipsis;
  - figures are whole, with their "$";
  - pending marks show whole, below their figure;
  - the current-week card is set apart by something other than background colour.
- **Mobile check:**
  - it passes on Analysis's walked states;
  - Analysis content-cell entries the diff turns green are deleted (`touch-target`, `text-size`, `h-scroll`, `name-overflow`, `figure-split`);
  - no entry is added outside gate.md's rules, and a failure that cannot be fixed is escalated;
  - if part 1 merged first, the offender JSON is rebased onto it and the check is rerun.
- **The PR attaches:**
  - the `mobile` e2e result and the offender counts from `scripts/design/compare.sh`;
  - the before and after phone captures of Analysis in both themes;
  - the unchanged `AnalisisCompu` capture.

#### Constraints carried from the request

- Every new phone rule sits under 900 px.
- Only the Analysis screen files and the Analysis block of the styles change.
- `routes.ts` routes, `kind` and `parent` do not change.
- Cells of other screens, and the bar cells on Analysis, are not touched.

### Questions for the human

None. UX Lead and UI Lead marked no finding as blocking.

### Observations on the design

- **Flow: way in.** On the phone, Analysis is reached from More, so it takes 2 touches from another tab, not 1.
- **Flow step 1 / month selector.** The drawing does draw the selector first, so the flow's caveat is wrong. The drawn arrows are 40×40, and the design grows them to 44×44.
- **Flow step 2.** The flow does not say what the next-month arrow does at the current month.
- **Flow step 3.** The flow leaves out the month summary block. The screens design keeps it unchanged.
- **Flow step 3.** The "no best week" line belongs under the week section, not the variety section.
- **Flow step 4.** It has a garbled sentence about the three withdrawal-type figures.
- **Week and month cards.** They are not drawn. They are built from the variety-card pattern and judged against the PRD.
- **Week card headline.** It is given as a range (15–20 px). UI Lead recommends the variety card's 20 px.
- **Current-week edge.** It is not drawn. It must stay under 900 px so the computer row does not change.
- **Best-week summary line.** The drawing has a summary line the product lacks, and adding it would be new copy. Only the existing `mejorSemana` mark and the "None stood out" line are kept.
- **Aside order.** The flow's order is wrong and leaves out the best-day line. The screens design follows the real order.
- **Month summary headline.** It is `.cifra-xl` (54 px) and will not hold a nine-digit figure on one line at 246 px. Use an existing smaller step.
- **Colours.** The screens design gives hex values. The build uses tokens.
- **60-character name.** It cannot be read in full on Analysis. Varieties shows it in full.
- **Error / no connection.** There is no drawing for it. The design reuses the shared notice.

## Interview: Tech Lead efímero→Tech Lead

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1: Analysis on the phone

I read `Analisis.tsx`, `useViewport.ts`, the Analysis and `.gastos__mes` blocks of `app.css`, the offender lists and `Analisis.test.tsx`. I could not run the declared checks: this role may not run commands that write.

**Where the current solution fails.** `Analisis.tsx` already renders both layouts in the DOM. The variety cards (`.analisis__variedades`) and the tables (`.analisis__tabla-contenedor`) are both emitted. `app.css` at line 3369 hides the cards with `display:none` at ≥900 px, and the tables are hidden below that. The PRD forbids exactly this: one layout in the DOM, no figure exposed twice, "no table in the page" on the phone. Finishing the week and month cards the way the variety cards were built would put every figure in the DOM twice. That breaks three acceptance tests: no table on the phone, no card on the computer, and no figure exposed twice to assistive technology. The existing tests (`.analisis__semana`, `.analisis__mes-fila`, `.analisis__tabla-contenedor--semanas`) query both layouts at once, so they prove nothing about "one at a time".

❓ **Q1** - **How is one layout chosen at a time?**: `useViewport()` (`web/src/shell/useViewport.ts`) is the one module that calls `matchMedia`, at the same 900 px as the CSS. The obvious plan is to branch inside `AnalisisContenido` on `useViewport()` and render either `<VarietyCards/>` or `<VarietyTable/>`, per section. Three things can go wrong:
- The CSS `display:none` rules at lines 3053 and 3369 stay dead and misleading.
- The tests need `test-viewport.ts` to set the viewport. They must fail if both layouts render.
- `useSyncExternalStore` renders `'phone'` when `matchMedia` is missing. In jsdom the default would be cards, so every existing table test would silently move to the phone.
➡️ Branch on `useViewport()` once, in `AnalisisContenido`. Delete the `display:none` pairs. Split into `AnalisisTablas.tsx` (computer markup, untouched as an element tree) and `AnalisisTarjetas.tsx`. State which layout the existing tests exercise, and add one test per section that asserts the absent layout (`queryByRole('table')` on the phone, no card class on the computer).

❓ **Q2** - **How is "AnalisisCompu unchanged" proved?**: The PRD demands a byte-for-byte unchanged capture. Today the computer tree also contains the hidden card markup. Removing it changes the computer DOM, even if the pixels are the same. The tables sit in `.analisis__principal`, and the cards are siblings in the same wrapper.
➡️ Capture the computer DOM (`outerHTML` of `.analisis__columnas`) before and after, and keep a test that snapshots the computer tree with the cards gone. Say in the plan which diff in the computer DOM you accept: only the removal of the card siblings. Do not edit any CSS rule that a ≥900 px selector uses beyond deleting the `display:none` pair.

❓ **Q3** - **Month selector first and the `order` leak**: `MonthNav` reuses the classes `gastos__mes*` from Expenses. The DOM already places it first in the fragment. The leak is `.gastos__mes { order: 2 }` (line 1511, inside a media query) and the base rule at 1281. Scoping it to Expenses by renaming or by a `.gastos .gastos__mes` selector also touches the Expenses block, but the request says to touch only the Analysis files and the Analysis block of `app.css`. The same class also carries the 44 px touch-target rules at lines 4584 and 4664 for `.gastos__mes-boton`, which Analysis depends on for its arrow offenders.
➡️ Give Analysis its own `analisis__mes` classes in `MonthNav`. Do not edit the Expenses rules at all, whether or not part 1 has merged. That makes the "if part 1 already scoped it" condition irrelevant. In return, state which rules `analisis__mes-boton` must copy for the 44×44 target (4584, 4664), so the arrow's target isn't lost with the shared class.

❓ **Q4** - **One shared card, or three?**: Variety, week and month cards share a shape: a headline figure (Sales), a truncating title and an ordered list of labelled figures, each with an optional pending mark below. The existing variety card is hand-written inline. Doing it three times gives three versions of "the figure never splits, the pending mark goes below", which is the figure-split offender today (7 entries).
➡️ One `StatCard` component (`title`, `headline`, `rows: {label, value, pending?}[]`, `current?`). It owns the `white-space: nowrap` figure, the "$" tied to its number (a non-breaking space or one nowrap span) and the pending line. Each of the three sections only maps its row to `rows`, in the row's order. The tests for the order can then be one parametrised test.

❓ **Q5** - **Which seams, and how does the work split?**: The 60-character name and nine-digit figures at 320 and 246 px can only be proved by the Mobile check (e2e, Playwright), not by jsdom. The offender lists are 31 Analysis entries across 8 rules (`touch-action` 8, `figure-split` 7, `text-size` 5, `touch-target` 5, `name-overflow` 3, plus the bar and chrome entries that belong to part 1). Some of those touch the aside (weekday bars, expense breakdowns), which stays a table-less list and gets no card.
➡️ Name the seam for each part: a pure `rowsOf(week)`, `rowsOf(month)` and `rowsOf(variety)` function with unit tests, the `useViewport` branch with Testing Library, and e2e for fit. List which entries each piece deletes, to keep the diff reviewable. Which piece would carry more than about four production modules plus tests? I expect `StatCard` plus the three section components, the aside fixes and the CSS block to be about that. If so, split into (a) the viewport branch plus the variety section and `StatCard`, (b) the weeks and months, (c) the aside fit and the offender deletions.

### Invariants proposed

- Analysis renders either its tables or its cards in the DOM, chosen by `useViewport()`, and never hides one layout with CSS.
- A figure's pending mark, and its "$" with its number, are rendered only by the shared card component (`StatCard`), never by a section's own markup.
- Analysis's month selector uses classes of its own, and a diff of Analysis does not edit any `.gastos__*` rule.

### Estimate

The Tech Lead has not given an estimate yet; please include one with the files you expect to touch. For reference, I read `.agentic/tasks/`. GH-86 closed at `cost_usd` 169.4 with a long run of subtasks, at about 1.4 to 3.7 each, so a UI task with 3 pieces and two e2e rounds is unlikely to come in below the low single digits per subtask plus review. A plan that touches Analisis.tsx (about 520 lines), a new card, an aside fix, the CSS and 31 offender entries is at the edge of the size limit and probably needs the split in Q5 before it builds.

### Round 1: answers

### Answers, round 1

What I read: `Analisis.tsx` (623 lines), `useViewport.ts`, `test-viewport.ts`, the `.gastos__mes*` rules in `app.css` (1281–1312, 1511–1516, and the shared `:active` / `user-select` lists at 4584 and 4664), the Analysis offender entries, and the `Analisis` rows in `scripts/design/screens.json`.

**Q1** (one layout at a time): **Accepted**, with three additions.
- **One branch.** `AnalisisContenido` calls `useViewport()` once. It then renders either `<AnalisisTablas …/>` or `<AnalisisTarjetas …/>` inside `.analisis__principal`.
  - The new files sit in `web/src/screens/`, as the request scopes.
  - `AnalisisTablas.tsx` is today's three table blocks and `CostSubRow`, moved without changing a single element or class.
- **Shared elements.** These stay in `AnalisisContenido` and render the same element at both widths, so crossing 900 px keeps them:
  - `MonthNav`;
  - the hero;
  - the three section headings (`porVariedad`, `semanaASemanaUltimas`, `mesAMesUltimos`);
  - the empty-month line;
  - the aside.
- **"No best week" line.** Today it renders only inside `.analisis__tabla-contenedor--semanas`, so the phone never shows it. That is a PRD gap this change closes.
  - On the computer it stays where it is, so the capture does not move.
  - On the phone it renders after the week cards.
  - Each layout owns its own copy of that one line.
- **Dead CSS.** Delete the `display:none` pairs that hide one layout per width (the base rule at 3053–3055 and the one for `.analisis__tabla-contenedor`, plus their ≥900 counterparts at 3369–3375). No other ≥900 declaration changes.
- **Tests.**
  - jsdom has no `matchMedia`, so today every Analysis test silently renders as `'phone'`.
  - From now on, every test in `Analisis.test.tsx` sets its viewport explicitly through `stubViewport` / `renderWithViewport` (`test-viewport.ts`). It never mocks `useViewport`.
  - The tests that query `.analisis__tabla-contenedor--*` (around lines 448 and 456) move to `'computer'`. The ones that read `.analisis__semana` / `.analisis__mes-fila` stay on `'phone'` and are re-pointed at the card markup.
  - Each section gets an "absent layout" assertion: `queryByRole('table')` is null on the phone, and no card is present on the computer.
  - One `flipViewport` test checks that the month selector, the headings and the aside are the same node objects before and after the flip.

**Q2** (proving `AnalisisCompu` is unchanged): **Partly overruled.**
- **No snapshot test.** I will not add an `outerHTML` snapshot test of the computer tree. Standards §5: a test that breaks on a refactor without a change of behaviour is wrong, and a markup snapshot is exactly that.
- **The capture is the proof.** The `AnalisisCompu` and `AnalisisCompuOscuro` captures, taken before and after and attached to the PR, are the evidence the request asks for.
- **Behaviour is pinned by Testing Library** at `'computer'`:
  - the three tables render;
  - their headers and cells keep their order;
  - no card is present.
- **The one accepted change to the computer DOM** is the removal of the card siblings (`.analisis__variedades`, `.analisis__semanas`, `.analisis__meses`), which today are `display:none` there.
- **CSS limit.** No rule that applies at ≥900 px changes, beyond deleting those hide rules. The plan says this, and the reviewer checks the `app.css` diff against it.

**Q3** (month selector first): **Overruled.** Analysis keeps the shared `gastos__mes*` classes.
- **Why not new classes.**
  - Renaming would drop `.gastos__mes-boton` out of the shared `:active` list (4584) and the `user-select` / `touch-action` list (4664).
  - Getting it back means editing those shared lists. That is an edit outside the Analysis block, and a larger one than the scoping the request explicitly sanctions.
  - It would also open new `touch-action` and `user-select` failures on the arrows.
- **Instead, scope only the leaking declaration**, as the request says.
  - The base `.gastos__mes { order: 3 }` (1282) moves to an Expenses-only selector: `.gastos > .gastos__mes`, or Expenses' own container, whichever matches its markup.
  - `flex-basis`, the height and the rest stay shared.
  - The ≥900 rule at 1511 (`order: 2`) is not touched, so neither computer capture can move (invariant 9).
  - If part 1 has already merged that scoping, this part skips it and says so in the PR.
- **44×44 arrows.**
  - The base `.gastos__mes-boton` is 40×40.
  - Analysis gets its own rule in the Analysis block, under `@media (max-width: 899.98px)`: `.analisis .gastos__mes-boton { min-width: 44px; min-height: 44px }`.
  - Expenses' own arrow offenders stay where they are; they are not this task's cells.
- **Your proposed invariant 3** becomes: "a diff of Analysis edits no `.gastos__*` rule except moving the base `order: 3` under an Expenses-only selector."

**Q4** (one card component or three): **Accepted**, with these details.
- **The component.** One `AnalisisTarjeta.tsx` (StatCard in your wording). Its props:
  - `title`: truncates with an ellipsis;
  - `headline`: Sales, `white-space: nowrap`;
  - `rows: { label: string; figure: string; pending: string }[]`, in order;
  - `current?: boolean`;
  - `mark?: string`: the best-week mark.
- **What it owns.**
  - **Figures never break.** Every `figure` is one nowrap span, so "$" and its number never split. I will not touch `formatMoney`'s space: that is a shared formatter.
  - **Pending mark.** It goes on its own line below its figure, never truncated.
  - **Current week.** It gets a `--actual` modifier that draws an edge (an inline-start border in `--tinta`), not just a fill. No new copy.
- **Labels.** Each section maps its row with the existing copy keys only.
  - Variety: `delMes`, `soldSuffix`, `regalados`, `costo`, `margen`, as the phone card uses them today.
  - Weeks and months: `COST_OF_GOODS_LABEL`, `SHORTFALL_LABEL`, `PROMOTION_LABEL`, `OWN_USE_LABEL`, `gastos`, `ganancia`, `caja`.
  - Cost figures come from the existing `rowCostParts`.
- **Order** follows the PRD's card lists. For weeks and months that is: costs, then expenses, profit and cash. This is the order today's phone cards already use. It differs from the table's visual order (the cost sub-row comes last), and the PRD settles it.
- **Week cards keep their `Bar`**, the phone design's, as an `aria-hidden` element. The best-week mark keeps `BEST_WEEK_MARK`.
- **Invariant, as you proposed but scoped to the card layout:** "in the phone cards, a figure's pending mark and its '$' are rendered only by `AnalisisTarjeta`."
  - The aside keeps `AmountRow`, which already renders its own pending mark.
  - The aside does not become cards, per the PRD.

**Q5** (seams and split): **Partly accepted.**
- **Seams.**
  - **Mapping functions.** `varietyCard`, `weekCard` and `monthCard` are pure functions from an API row to the card props, with unit tests for order and optional rows. The rule for optional rows: shortfall, promotion and own use are absent when `null`; share and percentage are absent when `null`.
  - **Viewport branch.** Testing Library through the screen with `fetch` faked, as Q1 describes. One parametrised test covers order per section.
  - **Fit.** Covered by the Mobile check (e2e) over the seed's extreme data, at 320 px and at 246 px.
- **Split.** Two subtasks, not three. The check fails on an entry whose cell now passes, so each subtask deletes, in its own diff, every entry it turns green.
  - **(a) The layout.** The viewport branch, `AnalisisTablas.tsx`, `AnalisisTarjeta.tsx`, `AnalisisTarjetas.tsx` (all three sections), the card CSS in the Analysis block, the `order` scoping and the 44 px arrows, plus the tests.
    - It deletes the variety-name `name-overflow` entry, the card `figure-split` entries and the arrows' `touch-target` entries.
    - Splitting weeks and months from varieties would leave the screen with mixed layouts and no single "no table on the phone" test, so they stay together.
  - **(b) The aside and the evidence.** Aside fit: category names truncate, nine-digit figures stay whole, no text under 12 px. Then the remaining Analysis content entries (`h-scroll`, both category `name-overflow` entries, `text-size`, any remaining `figure-split`), the rebase onto part 1's lists if it merged first, and the captures and counts for the PR.
- **Offender ownership.**
  - The `touch-action`, `chrome-at-load`, `chrome-mid-scroll` and `aria-busy` entries on Analysis are bar, floating-button and loading cells. They belong to part 1, and this part does not touch them.
  - If one of them is a content cell this diff turns green, it is deleted as well (invariant 8).
  - If a failure cannot be fixed, it is escalated, never listed.

### Estimate
Two subtasks plus review.
- **Files:**
  - `web/src/screens/Analisis.tsx` (shrinks);
  - new `AnalisisTablas.tsx`, `AnalisisTarjetas.tsx` and `AnalisisTarjeta.tsx`, plus a small pure module for the card mappings with its test;
  - `Analisis.test.tsx`;
  - the Analysis block of `web/src/styles/app.css`, plus the one `.gastos__mes` line;
  - `figure-split`, `name-overflow`, `touch-target`, `text-size` and `h-scroll` in `web/src/mobile-standard/offenders/`.
- **Cost:** about $3–5 for (a), $2–4 for (b), and about $2 for review and the captures. Roughly $8–11 in total.

### Where new code coexists with old shapes
- Analysis keeps the Spanish class and copy-key names (`gastos__mes*`, `ANALYSIS_TEXT.vendisteEnElMes` and the like). The new components carry English props, but they keep the existing BEM class family so the shared CSS lists keep applying.
- The aside keeps `AmountRow` and is not moved onto the new card.

### Round 2: questions

### Round 2: Analysis on the phone

I accept your Q1, Q2 (no snapshot test) and Q4 (one card) as stated. I read the `gastos__mes*` rules, `Gastos.tsx` 175–190, `Analisis.tsx` 245–530, `test-viewport.ts` and the offender entries (`touch-action`, `touch-target`) for Analysis and Gastos. Expenses and Analysis arrows share two lists, so Q1 below is where the plan breaks first.

**Where the plan fails now.** Your arrow fix and your `order` scoping name two selectors that match nothing in this tree:
- `.gastos > .gastos__mes`: in `Gastos.tsx` the month block is a child of `.gastos__cabecera`, not of `.gastos`. The selector would never apply, so Expenses would lose `order: 3` and `flex-basis: 100%` and its layout would move.
- `.analisis .gastos__mes-boton`: no element of Analysis carries an `analisis` class. The roots I found are `main.pantalla` and `.analisis__columnas`. The 44 px rule would never apply, and the two arrow `touch-target` entries would stay red.

❓ **Q1** - **Which selectors, and what do the arrows' entries become?**: Both fixes need a real hook.
- **Scoping.** The base `.gastos__mes` also carries `flex-basis: 100%`, not just `order`. In Analysis the parent is not `.gastos__cabecera`, so check whether that 100% is a height in a column flex, or inert.
- **Hook.** Analysis needs an ancestor class. `Analisis.tsx` is in scope, so you can add `analisis` to its content wrapper.
- **The `touch-action` entries.** You said the shared lists at 4584 and 4664 already hold `.gastos__mes-boton`. Yet `touch-action.json` lists `button:Next month` and `button:Previous month` for Analysis, Gastos and the Expense screens (issue 48). So either 4664 does not cover `touch-action` for these buttons, or it is under a rule that does not reach them. Also, `touch-action` is not one of the five rules the request lets this part delete.
- **A hazard.** Gastos shares the class. If the 44 px fix lands on the shared base `.gastos__mes-boton`, the Gastos and Gastos-state `touch-target` entries turn green and the check fails on a stale entry, in cells of screens this part excludes.
➡️ Say in the plan:
  - the exact selector for the Expenses-only `order`, which is `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes`;
  - the Analysis hook class;
  - the 44 px rule, scoped under that hook and the phone breakpoint;
  - what happens to Analysis's two `touch-action` arrow entries. If your diff turns them green, delete them (invariant 8); if not, they stay with issue 48. State which one it is after reading 4664.

❓ **Q2** - **Does the card row shape fit the existing copy keys?**: Your `rows: { label, figure, pending }` assumes "label, then figure". The keys you named are not uniform:
- Suffix style, the figure comes first: `delMes`, `soldSuffix`, `regalados`, and `ventas: ' sales'`.
- Prefix style, the label comes first: `costo`, `margen`, `gastos: 'expenses '`, `ganancia: 'profit '`, `caja: 'cash '`.
- The spaces are baked into the strings (leading or trailing). In a flex row, those spaces collapse and the label glues to the figure.
So one `label` field either reorders a row (breaking "same order") or forces you to strip the whitespace, which is a copy change. And it does not say what a 246 px row does: if the label and a nowrap nine-digit figure ("$ 999.999.999") cannot share a line, the figure overflows and `h-scroll` fails.
➡️ Make each row `{ before, figure, after, pending }`, or use `COMMON_TEXT` header keys (`sales`, `grams`, `expenses`, `profit`, `cash`, which have no baked spaces and are the labels the table already shows) for weeks and months. Let the row wrap (`flex-wrap: wrap`) so the label sits above the figure when the line is full, and never `nowrap` the whole row. Add a 246 px e2e row with nine-digit figures for each of the three card kinds.

❓ **Q3** - **Does subtask (a) fit the cap?**: (a) carries these production modules:
- `Analisis.tsx` (branch);
- `AnalisisTablas.tsx`;
- `AnalisisTarjeta.tsx`;
- `AnalisisTarjetas.tsx`;
- the mapping module;
- `app.css`.
That is six, and the first is a moved block of about 200 lines (the three tables and `CostSubRow`) that must keep its element tree. This is the size where a Developer runs out of turns mid-way, with a half-moved computer tree.
➡️ Split (a) into (a1), a pure refactor that proves the computer is unchanged, and (a2), which adds the phone cards.
  - **(a1):** the `useViewport` branch rendering `AnalisisTablas` for both widths for now, the extraction, the viewport-explicit tests at `'computer'`, and the deletion of the dead `display:none` rules. It touches no phone behaviour, and its diff can be reviewed against the computer capture on its own.
  - **(a2):** the mapping module, `AnalisisTarjeta`, `AnalisisTarjetas`, the card CSS and the arrow fixes, with the phone tests and the entry deletions.
  - (b) stays as it is. That makes three subtasks, each under about four modules.

❓ **Q4** - **Where do the "no best week" line and the empty month go, so the shared elements stay shared?**: You moved the "no best week" line out of `.analisis__tabla-contenedor--semanas` for the phone and said each layout owns its copy. Two copies of one sentence means two places to change, and the flip test only covers the headings, the selector and the aside. The empty-month line already lives in `AnalisisContenido` (line 310), but it is inside the `m.varieties.length === 0` ternary that also wraps both layouts.
➡️ Render the "no best week" line once in `AnalisisContenido`, after the layout, at both widths, and move it out of the computer table block. This moves the computer DOM by one node, so say so in the invariant list for (a1) and check it by eye in the capture, since the PRD says the computer is unchanged. If you refuse because of the capture, keep the line inside the table block and make `AnalisisTarjetas` call the same `NoBestWeekLine` component, so there is still one wording.

### Invariants proposed

- Every phone rule that Analysis sets on a shared `gastos__*` element is scoped under an Analysis-only ancestor class and the phone breakpoint, and no declaration of the base `.gastos__mes*` or `.gastos__mes-boton` rules is edited except moving `order: 3` under `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes`.
- A card row renders its label, figure and pending mark from one row object, with no spacing or order decided by whitespace in a copy string.
- A sentence that Analysis shows at both widths (the empty-month line and the "no best week" line) is rendered by one component, not copied into each layout.

### Estimate

The provisional $8–11 is not below what comparable work cost. In `.agentic/tasks/GH-86.md`, per-subtask `cost_usd` runs from about 1.4 to 3.7, so $3–5 for (a) and $2–4 for (b) is at or above the top of that range, with 2 subtasks. The risk is the other way: the Mobile check is an e2e loop that fails on a stale entry. Three subtasks (Q3) with that loop in (a2) and (b) will probably total above $11. If that pushes the task over the size limit, it will be split before it builds, so the split in Q3 is cheaper to decide now.

### Answers, round 2

What I read: `Gastos.tsx` 178–196, `Analisis.tsx` 590–623, `app.css` 1281–1312, 1511–1516 and 4584–4671, every `touch-action` rule in `app.css`, the arrow entries in `touch-action.json` and `touch-target.json`, and the copy keys in `copy/analysis.ts` and `copy/common.ts`.

**Q1** (selectors, hook class and the arrows' entries): **Accepted, with one correction to your selector.**
- **Order scoping.** The rule is `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes { order: 3 }`, placed **inside `@media (max-width: 899.98px)`**. `order: 3` is deleted from the base `.gastos__mes`.
  - The media query is needed. Your selector has specificity (0,2,0). Left unqualified, it would beat the ≥900 rule `.gastos__mes { order: 2 }` (0,1,0, line 1511), and the Expenses computer layout would change.
  - Inside the phone query, both screens keep exactly what they have today at both widths:
    - Expenses keeps `order: 3` on the phone and `order: 2` on the computer;
    - Analysis keeps `order: 2` on the computer and has no `order` on the phone.
  - Line 1511 is not edited.
- **`flex-basis: 100%` stays on the base rule.** Analysis has it today at both widths, so leaving it changes neither capture. Moving it would mean proving it does nothing in `.cuerpo`, and nothing gains from that.
- **Hook class.** `analisis` goes on Analysis's existing root: `<main className="pantalla analisis">`.
  - It adds no node, so the computer DOM keeps its structure.
  - No `.analisis` rule exists today, only `.analisis__*`, so the class styles nothing until this diff uses it.
- **Arrows.** One rule, in the Analysis block:
  `@media (max-width: 899.98px) { .analisis .gastos__mes-boton { min-width: 44px; min-height: 44px; touch-action: manipulation; } }`
  - The base `.gastos__mes-boton` is not touched. Gastos and its two state screens keep their entries.
- **The `touch-action` entries.**
  - Line 4664 is the `user-select: none` list only.
  - The only `touch-action: manipulation` rules in `app.css` are on `html` (line 23) and on `.cobro__metodo .selector-pago__opcion` (line 5469). `touch-action` is not inherited, so nothing reaches the arrows today. That is why they are listed.
  - The rule above turns Analysis's two arrow `touch-action` entries green, so this diff deletes them (invariant 8). The same holds for Analysis's two arrow `touch-target` entries.
  - The Gastos, GastoEditar and GastoConfirmarBorrado arrow entries stay with issue 48.

**Q2** (card rows and the copy keys): **Accepted.** The row is `{ before, figure, after, pending }`. The row does not wrap with flex.
- **Why not `COMMON_TEXT` headers.** I keep the phone drawing's wording that the phone cards use today (`costo`, `margen`, `gastos`, `ganancia`, `caja`, `delMes`, `soldSuffix`, `regalados`). The `COMMON_TEXT` headers are the computer table's. Both are existing copy, and the phone design governs the phone.
- **Spaces.** A row is a block box with inline content: `before`, then the figure, then `after`. The figure is the only `white-space: nowrap` span.
  - Spaces baked into the copy stay significant in inline flow, so no copy string changes.
  - When the line is full, the label wraps above or before the figure. The figure itself never breaks, and "$" stays with its number.
- **Pending mark.** `pending` renders as its own block line below, never truncated or nowrapped.
- **Names and period labels** are the only things with `overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis`.
- **Margin percentage.** It goes in `after`, built the way the phone card builds it today. No new key.
- **Proof at 246 px.** The Mobile check is the e2e: it walks Analysis at 320 px and 246 px over the seed's extreme data. I add no separate Playwright spec.
  - (a2) must confirm in `scripts/design/mobile_seed.py` that a variety, a week and a month each carry a nine-digit figure and a pending mark.
  - If one kind lacks them, the subtask escalates. It does not change the seed, because the seed is the pinned fixture the lists are valid against (ADR-0048).
  - On top of that, a jsdom test checks that the class tree is right: the figure is in the nowrap span and the pending mark is outside it.

**Q3** (does subtask (a) fit the cap): **Accepted, with one change to (a1).** Three subtasks.
- **(a1) A pure refactor.**
  - `Analisis.tsx` branches on `useViewport()` once and gets the `analisis` root class.

**Q4** ("no best week" line and empty month): **Your fallback.** One component, rendered at the table's current place on the computer.

### Round 3: questions

### Round 3: Analysis on the phone

I accept all of round 2's answers, including the `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes` selector inside the phone query and the `analisis` class on `<main>`. I read `Analisis.tsx` 78–160 and 392–470, the Analysis entries of `text-size`, `figure-split`, `h-scroll`, `name-overflow` and `aria-busy`, the `Analisis` rows of `screens.json` and the references in `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts`.

**Where the plan fails now.** Your (b) says it deletes the "remaining `text-size`" entries. Every one of Analysis's five `text-size` entries is a bar label: `span:Customers`, `Home`, `More`, `Sales`, `Varieties`. Those belong to part 1, and the request forbids touching them. So (b) has no `text-size` entry to delete, and a Developer told to delete them will either touch bar cells or add a rule that does nothing. Round 2 also left some cells with no owner, and the two new files from (a2) overlap (b) on the same JSON files (Q2 and Q4).

❓ **Q1** - **Do the cards go through `AmountRow`, or through your row object?**: The phone cards today render their cost lines with `RowCostLines` and `WithdrawalLines`, which render `AmountRow`. The computer's `CostSubRow` (line 142) uses the same `RowCostLines`, and the hero uses `WithdrawalLines variant="row"`. `AmountRow` draws its own pending mark. You said the card rows come from `rowCostParts` into `{ before, figure, after, pending }`. If a card still renders `<RowCostLines>`, the pending mark and the figure are drawn by `AmountRow`, outside your `StatCard`, so the invariant "one row object" is false. And any card-fit CSS written against `.analisis__costos` or `AmountRow`'s compact variant reaches the computer sub-row, which the capture must not move.
➡️ In the cards, map `rowCostParts(...)` and `amountParts(...)` into the card's rows and do not render `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`. They stay for the computer, the hero and the aside. Say so in (a2).

❓ **Q2** - **Who owns each Analysis entry?**: I mapped them:
- `name-overflow`: two `Categoría Larguísimo…` spans (the aside's expense breakdowns) and the variety name. Only the variety name is a card cell (a2).
- `figure-split`: seven entries, `$ #.#` at 320, 375 and 430 px, `$ #.#.#.#[1]` and `[2]`, `$ #.#.#[1]` and `[2]`, and `$ #.#[1]` and `[2]` at 246 px. I cannot tell from the file which are cards, which are the hero (`cifra-xl`, the grams line, `WithdrawalLines variant="row"`) and which are the aside. The hero is in neither (a2) nor (b) as you wrote them.
- `h-scroll`: one page-level entry with no target. It turns green only when every cell is fixed, so it belongs to the last subtask that runs.
- `aria-busy`: one entry. It is not in the request's five rules.
➡️ Give (b) the hero and the "since the first record" and withdrawal lines as well as the aside, say that (b) deletes no `text-size` entry, and say that `aria-busy` stays (issue 48) unless your diff turns it green. In (a1), before writing any card, run the Mobile check and record which target belongs to which cell, so (a2) and (b) get an exact list rather than a guess. If a cell cannot be mapped, escalate in (a1).

❓ **Q3** - **Where do the PRD's fit checks live?**: Two of your proofs are weak.
- A jsdom test that "the figure is in the nowrap span and the pending mark is outside it" is a class-tree test. It breaks if the markup is refactored with no change in behaviour, which §5 calls wrong. It also proves nothing about layout, because jsdom has no layout.
- The Mobile check has no rule for "the pending mark is on the line below its figure", "the current week is set apart by shape" or "the ellipsis is on the name". `name-overflow` and `figure-split` cover the second and the first only for the cells they walk. Analysis's only walked rows are `Analisis` and `AnalisisOscuro` (the default month). Whether that month has a pending mark, a current week, an empty month or "no best week" depends on `scripts/design/mobile_seed.py`. You left the seed check to (a2), which is after the cards are built.
➡️ Replace the class-tree test with a behaviour test: inside each card's group, the figure's text is exactly `formatMoney(...)`, the pending text is in a different element from it, and the order of the figures is the row's. Put the layout claims in a phone Playwright spec in `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts` (extreme data, 320 and 246 px): the pending line sits below its figure (its top is greater than the figure's bottom), the name's `scrollWidth` exceeds its `clientWidth`, and the current card's computed border differs from its siblings'. Do the seed check in (a1) and escalate there if a kind lacks a pending figure.

❓ **Q4** - **Do (a2) and (b) run in parallel?**: Both edit the Analysis block of `app.css` and both delete entries from `figure-split.json`, `name-overflow.json` and `h-scroll.json`. Two branches editing the same JSON and the same CSS block will conflict when the second one merges, and the second Developer sees only its own branch.
➡️ Make the order explicit: (a1), then (a2), then (b), each rebased on the one before. If you want (b) in parallel, give it its own CSS block (`/* Analysis aside */`) and its own entries only (the aside, hero and withdrawal lines), and say that `h-scroll` is deleted only by the last to merge.

### Invariants proposed

- The phone cards render no `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`: their cost figures come from `rowCostParts` and `amountParts` through the card's row object.
- A diff of Analysis deletes none of the bar-label or floating-button entries of Analysis (`text-size` on `Customers`, `Home`, `More`, `Sales`, `Varieties`, and the `touch-action` and `chrome-*` entries of the bar), and adds none.

### Estimate

$11–14 is not below the closed tasks. GH-86's per-subtask `cost_usd` runs from about 1.4 to 3.7, so $4–5 for (a2) is above that range, and three subtasks plus review at that rate is in line with it. The risk is a rerun: if the entry mapping in Q2 is guessed wrong, (a2) fails on a stale entry and pays for a second pass. The mapping in (a1) is the cheapest thing in the plan to decide first.

### Answers, round 3

What I read: the head of `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts` and its tests at lines 24, 183 and 283–304. The second test builds fully and partly pending data through the built front against the real backend, and screenshots phone and computer by looking up `.analisis__semanas` / `.analisis__tabla-contenedor--semanas` (line 294).

**Q1** (do the cards go through `AmountRow`?): **Accepted.**
- **What (a2) renders.** In the phone cards, every cost and withdrawal figure is mapped from `rowCostParts(...)` / `amountParts(...)` into the card's `{ before, figure, after, pending }` rows. The cards do not render `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`.
- **What stays unchanged.** Those three components keep serving the computer's `CostSubRow`, the hero and the aside.
- **CSS.** The card CSS uses only the card's own classes (`analisis__tarjeta*`) under `.analisis` and the phone query. It never targets `.analisis__costos` or `AmountRow`'s classes, so the computer sub-row cannot move.
- **(a1) does not apply this.** Its phone branch keeps today's markup, `RowCostLines` included, because it is a pure refactor.
- **Your invariant is adopted as written.**

**Q2** (who owns each Analysis entry?): **Accepted.**
- **(a1) maps the entries first.** Before any card exists, it runs the Mobile check on the refactored tree and writes, in its PR, every Analysis entry with the cell it belongs to. The cells are: card, hero, since-the-first-record, withdrawal line, aside, bar or floating button.
  - If an entry cannot be mapped, (a1) escalates.
  - (a2) and (b) then delete from that list, not from a guess.
- **(b) owns more than the aside.** It owns the hero (`cifra-xl`, the grams line, the hero's `WithdrawalLines`), the "since the first record" block, both expense breakdowns with their two `Categoría Larguísimo…` `name-overflow` entries, and the weekday bars.
- **No `text-size` entry is deleted.** All five are bar labels (`Customers`, `Home`, `More`, `Sales`, `Varieties`) and belong to part 1. I withdraw "remaining `text-size`" from (b).
  - The 12 px floor still applies to every Analysis content cell (b) touches.
  - A content text under 12 px that the check does not list is fixed, not listed.
- **`aria-busy` stays with issue 48**, unless a diff turns it green, in which case that diff deletes it (invariant 8).
- **`h-scroll`** has no target and is page-level, so it is deleted only by the subtask after which the check passes for the page. By the order in Q4, that is (b).
- **Your second invariant is adopted:** no diff of this part deletes or adds a bar-label, floating-button, `touch-action` bar or `chrome-*` entry of Analysis.

**Q3** (where the fit checks live): **Accepted.**
- **jsdom test.** The class-tree test is dropped. In its place, a behaviour test inside each card's group (`role="group"`, named by its title) checks that:
  - each figure's text is exactly the formatted value;
  - each pending text is in a different element from its figure;
  - the figures follow the row's order.
- **Playwright.** The layout claims go in a new phone test in `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts`, at 320 px and at 246 px. It checks that:
  - the pending line's top is greater than its figure's bottom;
  - the variety name's `scrollWidth` exceeds its `clientWidth` and its computed `text-overflow` is `ellipsis`;
  - the current week card's computed border differs from its siblings';
  - `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` is at most `clientWidth`.
- **Where its data comes from.** The spec builds its own extreme data through the front against the real backend, as the test at line 183 already does:
  - a 60-character variety name;
  - prices and grams that give nine-digit figures;
  - a partly pending cost.

  So these claims do not depend on `mobile_seed.py`.
- **The seed check moves to (a1).** It reads `mobile_seed.py` to see which states the Mobile check itself walks for Analysis: a nine-digit figure and a pending mark on a variety, a week and a month. It records them in the PR.
  - If a kind lacks a state, (a1) escalates. The seed is not edited (ADR-0048).
  - The Playwright spec above still covers the claim either way.
- **Existing spec.** (a2) updates the existing spec's phone locators (line 294) to the card markup in the same diff.

**Q4** (do (a2) and (b) run in parallel?): **Strictly in sequence**: (a1), then (a2), then (b). Each is rebased on the one before. None runs in parallel.
- The CSS block and the three JSON files are shared, and the entry map from (a1) feeds both later subtasks.
- (b) is the last to merge, so it deletes `h-scroll`, and only if the page passes at 320 px and at 246 px.
- (b) also does the rebase onto part 1, if part 1 merged, and reruns the check against the merged lists.

### Closing

### Closing summary

This is the last round, so I close with what the interview settled and what stays risky. I found no new flaw in round 3's answers that changes the plan. I did not run the declared checks (this role writes nothing), so the risks below are reasoned from the code.

**Decided**
- **One layout in the DOM.** `AnalisisContenido` branches once on `useViewport()`.
  - The computer renders `AnalisisTablas.tsx` (the tables and `CostSubRow`, moved without changing an element).
  - The phone renders cards.
  - The `display:none` pairs are deleted.
  - The month selector, the headings, the aside and the empty-month line are shared nodes.
  - `NoBestWeekLine` is one component, rendered where the computer table has it today.
- **Month selector.**
  - `order: 3` moves to `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes` inside the phone query.
  - Line 1511 and the base `.gastos__mes-boton` are untouched.
  - `<main className="pantalla analisis">` is the hook.
  - The 44 px and `touch-action` rule on the arrows lives under `.analisis` in the phone query.
- **Cards.**
  - One `AnalisisTarjeta`, with rows `{ before, figure, after, pending }` mapped from `rowCostParts` and `amountParts`.
  - No `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines` inside a card.
  - The figure is the only nowrap span, the pending mark is its own line, and only names and period labels get an ellipsis.
  - The current week is set apart by an edge.
- **Tests.**
  - Every Analysis test sets its viewport explicitly.
  - Behaviour tests check each card's figures, their order and a separate pending element.
  - The layout claims go in a phone Playwright test with its own extreme data.
  - No markup snapshot of the computer tree: the capture is the proof.
- **Split.** Three subtasks, in sequence, each rebased on the one before:
  - **(a1)** the refactor, the viewport tests, the entry map and the seed reading;
  - **(a2)** the cards, the card CSS, the arrows and the new e2e;
  - **(b)** the hero, the aside, since-the-first-record fit, the remaining deletions, the rebase onto part 1 and the PR evidence.
- **Offender lists.**
  - (b) deletes `h-scroll` last.
  - No `text-size` entry is deleted (all five are bar labels).
  - Bar, floating-button and `chrome-*` entries are left alone.
  - `aria-busy` stays unless a diff turns it green.

**Known risks**
- **The entry map is the weakest point.** `figure-split`, `name-overflow` and `h-scroll` targets are anonymous (`$ #.#.#[1]`). If (a1) cannot map a target to a cell, (a2) can fail on a stale entry and pay a second pass.
- **Cards at 246 px.** A label plus a nine-digit nowrap figure may not fit one line. The plan lets the label wrap above the figure, but only the e2e at 246 px proves that, not jsdom.
- **Computer capture.** It must stay unchanged by eye. The one accepted change to the computer DOM is the removal of the hidden card siblings, and a reviewer must check that the `app.css` diff touches no rule that applies at 900 px and above, beyond deleting the hide rules.
- **Part 1 merge order.** If part 1 merges first, (b) rebases the offender JSON and the `.gastos__mes` lines. A conflict in `app.css` or the lists is likely, and (b) has to resolve it.
- **Seed coverage.** If `mobile_seed.py` lacks a pending figure or a current week for one card kind, the Mobile check will not prove it. The plan escalates in (a1) and leaves the proof to the new Playwright test.
- **Cost.** The estimate of about $11–14 for three subtasks plus review is not below what GH-86's subtasks cost (about $1.4–3.7 each). It is likely to cross the size limit if the entry map is wrong, so the split above should hold.

### Invariants proposed

- Every phone rule Analysis sets on a shared `gastos__*` element is under `.analisis` and the phone query, and the only edit to the shared month rules is moving `order: 3` to `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes` inside the phone query.
- A card row renders from one `{ before, figure, after, pending }` object, with the figure as the only nowrap span and the pending mark on its own line.
- A sentence Analysis shows at both widths is rendered by one component.
- The phone cards render no `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`.
- A diff of this part deletes or adds none of Analysis's bar-label, floating-button, `touch-action` bar or `chrome-*` entries.

## Tech Lead

_the delivery tool was called 2 times over this run before it delivered_

_Reaped 2 background process(es): 2 term_

### Plan: Analysis on the phone (GH-319, part 2)

**Problem in the code today.** `Analisis.tsx` puts both layouts in the DOM: the phone's card blocks (`.analisis__variedades`, `.analisis__semanas`, `.analisis__meses`) and the computer's tables (`.analisis__tabla-contenedor--*`). `app.css` hides one of them per width with `display:none`, so every figure is in the DOM twice. On top of that:
- the base rule `.gastos__mes { order: 3 }` leaks into Analysis;
- the month arrows are 40 px wide and have no `touch-action`;
- the phone week and month blocks draw their figures through `AmountRow`, without the guarantees the PRD asks for: the figure never splits, the pending mark sits below it, and names end in an ellipsis.

**Shape of the solution.**
- **One layout at a time.** `AnalisisContenido` calls `useViewport()` once.
  - At computer width it renders `AnalisisTablas.tsx`: the three tables and `CostSubRow`, moved without changing a single element or class.
  - At phone width it renders `AnalisisTarjetas.tsx`.
- **Shared nodes, rendered at both widths:** the month selector, the hero, the three section headings, the empty-month line and the aside.
- **The "no best week" line** is one component, `NoBestWeekLine`.
  - The computer renders it where it is today, inside the week table container, so the capture does not move.
  - The phone renders it after the week cards.
- **One card component, `AnalisisTarjeta`.** It renders a title that truncates, the Sales headline and its rows, each row `{ before, figure, after, pending }`.
  - The figure is the only `white-space: nowrap` span.
  - The label wraps in inline flow, and the spaces baked into the copy keys are kept.
  - The pending mark is its own line below the figure.
  - The current week is set apart by an edge.
- **Mapping functions.** Pure `varietyCard`, `weekCard` and `monthCard` turn API rows into card props, using only existing copy keys and in the PRD's order.
  - They read `rowCostParts` and `amountParts`.
  - They never render `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`. Those stay for the computer, the hero and the aside.
- **CSS.**
  - `order: 3` moves from the base `.gastos__mes` to `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes`, inside `@media (max-width: 899.98px)`. Line 1511 is not edited.
  - `<main className="pantalla analisis">` is the hook for every phone rule Analysis adds.
  - The arrows get `min-width/min-height: 44px; touch-action: manipulation` under `.analisis` in the phone query.

**Why this split.** There are three subtasks, strictly in sequence, each rebased on the one before. They share the CSS block and the same offender JSON files, and the entry map from (a1) feeds both later subtasks.
- **(a1)** is a pure refactor, checked against the unchanged computer capture.
- **(a2)** builds the cards.
- **(b)** fixes the hero and the aside, deletes the remaining entries and assembles the PR evidence.

**Where new code coexists with old shapes.**
- Analysis keeps sharing the Spanish `gastos__mes*` classes and copy-key names with Expenses. The new phone rules reach them only through `.analisis`.
- The new components have English props and keep the `analisis__*` BEM family.
- The computer keeps `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` and `WithdrawalLines`. The phone cards use their own row object. The two shapes coexist on purpose, one per layout.

**Security and performance.** No input widens what it reaches. No data reaches the screen that did not already. Cost is linear in rows: at most 8 weeks, 12 months and a month's varieties, the same as today.

**Offender ownership.**
- This part never deletes or adds Analysis's bar-label `text-size` entries (Customers, Home, More, Sales, Varieties), the bar's `touch-action` entries or the `chrome-*` entries. Part 1 owns them.
- `aria-busy` stays with issue 48, unless a diff turns it green; that diff then deletes it.
- `h-scroll` is deleted only by (b), and only once the page passes at 320 px and at 246 px.
- A failure this part cannot fix is escalated, never listed.

**Estimate.** The total is about $28.
- About $14.4 is already spent on product definition and this interview.
- About $13–14 remains: (a1) about $3, (a2) about $5, (b) about $4, plus review and evidence.

**Known risks.**
- The `figure-split` targets carry no name (for example `$ #.#.#[1]`), so the entry map from (a1) is the weakest point.
- At 246 px only the e2e proves fit.
- If part 1 merges first, (b) faces a likely conflict in `app.css` and the offender lists.

### Subtasks

`### Subtasks` written by the system from the structured plan: no exact `### Subtasks` line (outside code fences) was found in this section, so the block below was built from the structured plan. Any list the Tech Lead wrote under another heading is not the one the Developers read.

#### Analysis: one layout in the DOM, chosen by useViewport, computer tables extracted unchanged

A pure refactor of `web/src/screens/Analisis.tsx`. Phone behaviour does not change. The computer capture stays identical.

**Build**
- Add the class `analisis` to the root: `<main className="pantalla analisis">`.
- In `AnalisisContenido`, call `useViewport()` (`web/src/shell/useViewport.ts`) once.
  - At `'computer'`, render the new `web/src/screens/AnalisisTablas.tsx`. It holds today's three table blocks (variety, weeks, months, around lines 349–525) and `CostSubRow`, moved without changing any element, class, id or attribute.
  - At `'phone'`, render today's phone markup exactly as it is: `.analisis__variedades`, `.analisis__semanas`, `.analisis__meses`, with their `RowCostLines`.
- Shared nodes stay in `AnalisisContenido` and are rendered at both widths:
  - `MonthNav`;
  - the hero;
  - the three section heading `pie` spans;
  - the aside.
- Empty-month line: split the `m.varieties.length === 0` ternary so `EMPTY_MONTH_LINE` is one shared node, and only the variety list or table sits inside the viewport branch.
- Extract `NoBestWeekLine` (exported from `Analisis.tsx`). It owns the condition (`weeks.length > 0 && no week is best`) and renders `NO_BEST_WEEK_LINE`.
  - `AnalisisTablas` renders it inside `.analisis__tabla-contenedor--semanas`, where it is today.
  - The phone markup renders it after the week blocks.
- In `web/src/styles/app.css`, delete the `display:none` rules that hide one layout per width: around 3053–3055 for the card containers, the phone hide of `.analisis__tabla-contenedor`, and their ≥900 counterparts around 3369–3375. Change no other declaration that applies at ≥900 px.

**Tests** (`web/src/screens/Analisis.test.tsx`)
- Every test sets its viewport through `stubViewport` / `renderWithViewport` from `web/src/test-viewport.ts`.
- Existing table tests (around lines 448 and 456) run at `'computer'`. Existing phone-block tests run at `'phone'`.
- Add:
  - at `'phone'`, `queryByRole('table')` is null;
  - at `'computer'`, the three tables render and no `.analisis__semana`, `.analisis__mes-fila` or `.analisis__variedad` is present;

- List every `Analisis` entry in `web/src/mobile-standard/offenders/*.json`. For each, name the cell it belongs to: variety card, week card, month card, month arrows, hero, since-the-first-record, withdrawal line, aside expense breakdown, weekday bars, bar or floating button.
- Read `scripts/design/mobile_seed.py` and record whether the walked Analysis month has, for a variety, a week and a month, a nine-digit figure and a pending mark. Record also whether it has a current week. Do not edit the seed.

**Acceptance**

- The `AnalisisCompu` and `AnalisisCompuOscuro` captures (`scripts/design/comparisons.mjs`) come out unchanged. Attach before and after.
- The only accepted change to the computer DOM is the removal of the hidden card siblings.

#### Analysis phone cards: one card component for varieties, weeks and months, month selector first, 44 px arrows

Builds on `layout-branch`. Replaces the phone markup it kept with cards. Read that subtask's entry map in its PR first.

**Mapping** (`web/src/screens/analisisCards.ts`, pure)
- `varietyCard`, `weekCard`, `monthCard` each take an API row (`web/src/api/analysis.ts` types) and return `{ title, headline, rows: { before, figure, after, pending }[], current?, mark? }`.
- Use existing copy keys only. Do not add or reword one.
  - Variety:
    - title: the name;
    - headline: `formatMoney(sales_without_shipping)`;
    - rows, in order:
      - share `{figure: share%, after: delMes}`, only when `share_percent` is not null;
      - grams sold with `COMMON_TEXT.soldSuffix`;
      - grams gifted with `regalados`;
      - cost: `before: costo`, with its pending text;
      - margin: `before: margen`, the ` (N%)` in `after` only when `percent` is not null, and its pending text.
  - Week and month:
    - title: `formatDateShort(first_day)` for a week, `formatMonthLong(month)` for a month;
    - headline: Sales;
    - rows, in order:
      - grams;
      - `COST_OF_GOODS_LABEL`;
      - `SHORTFALL_LABEL`, `PROMOTION_LABEL` and `OWN_USE_LABEL`, each only when not null;
      - `gastos` + expenses;
      - `ganancia` + profit, with its pending text;
      - `caja` + cash.
    - `current` is true for the last week. `mark` is `BEST_WEEK_MARK` when `best`.
- Figures and pending texts come from `rowCostParts` / `amountParts` (`web/src/screens/analisisCost.ts`) and the existing `pendingSuffix` wording.

**Card** (`web/src/screens/AnalisisTarjeta.tsx`)
- `role="group"`, named by its title.
- The title truncates with an ellipsis.
- The headline is one nowrap span.
- Each row is a block with inline content: `before`, the figure (the only `white-space: nowrap` span), then `after`. Spaces baked into the copy must stay significant.
- `pending` is its own block line below, never truncated.
- `current` adds a modifier that draws an edge (an inline-start border from a token), not only a fill.
- The week card keeps its `Bar` as `aria-hidden`, and `mark` shows the best-week mark.

**Sections** (`web/src/screens/AnalisisTarjetas.tsx`)
- Maps the three lists to cards and renders `NoBestWeekLine` after the week cards.
- Renders no `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`.
- `Analisis.tsx` renders it at `'phone'`, replacing the old phone markup. Remove that markup and any CSS that only it used, and only in phone-only rules.

**CSS** (`web/src/styles/app.css`)
- Card styles: `analisis__tarjeta*` classes under `.analisis`, inside `@media (max-width: 899.98px)`. Never target `.analisis__costos` or `AmountRow` classes. Colours, spacing and type come only from tokens. No text under 12 px.
- Month selector first:
  - delete `order: 3` from the base `.gastos__mes` (around line 1282);
  - add `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes { order: 3 }` inside the phone media query;
  - do not edit line 1511's rule or the base `.gastos__mes-boton`. If part 1 of GH-319 has already merged this scoping, skip it and say so.
- Arrows: `.analisis .gastos__mes-boton { min-width: 44px; min-height: 44px; touch-action: manipulation; }` inside the phone query.

**Tests**
- `web/src/screens/analisisCards.test.ts`, unit tests: row order per kind; optional rows absent when null (share, percent, shortfall, promotion, own use); current week; best mark.
- `Analisis.test.tsx` at `'phone'`:
  - the month selector is the first control (first button in document order is Previous month);
  - each section renders one group per row;
  - inside each group, each figure's text is exactly the formatted value, in the row's order, with the existing labels;
  - each pending text is in a different element from its figure;
  - no table is present.
- `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts`:
  - update the phone locators (around line 294) to the card markup;
  - add a phone test at 320 px and at 246 px, with data built through the front against the real backend (as the test around line 183 does): a 60-character variety name, prices and grams that give nine-digit figures, and a partly pending cost.
  - It asserts:
    - `documentElement.scrollWidth <= clientWidth`;
    - the name's `scrollWidth > clientWidth` and computed `text-overflow` is `ellipsis`;
    - each pending line's top is greater than its figure's bottom;
    - the current week card's computed border differs from its siblings'.

**Offenders**
- Delete the entries this diff turns green, using the entry map:
  - Analysis's `touch-target` and `touch-action` entries for `button:Next month` / `button:Previous month`;
  - the variety-name `name-overflow` entry;
  - the card `figure-split` entries.
- Do not touch Gastos/GastoEditar/GastoConfirmarBorrado arrow entries, bar or chrome entries, or `h-scroll`.
- The Mobile check passes.
- `AnalisisCompu` and `AnalisisCompuOscuro` stay unchanged. Attach the captures.

Seams: `analisisCards.ts pure functions (varietyCard, weekCard, monthCard)`, `Analisis screen with Testing Library, fetch faked, viewport via test-viewport.ts`, `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts phone test at 320 and 246 px`, `Mobile check (mobile project)`.

`web/src/screens/Analisis.test.tsx`, at the screen seam with `fetch` faked and the viewport set through `test-viewport.ts`:

- At `'phone'`, `queryByRole('table')` is null. At `'computer'`, three tables render and no `.analisis__variedad`, `.analisis__semana` or `.analisis__mes-fila` is present.
- With `flipViewport`, the month selector, the three section headings and the aside are the same node objects after crossing 900 px and back.
- The no-best-week line and the empty-month line each show exactly once at both widths.

### Entry map
Every `Analisis` entry in `web/src/mobile-standard/offenders/*.json`, mapped to its cell. "Aside" means the since-the-first-record expense breakdown and the month's expense breakdown (`.analisis__gastos`).

**figure-split** (issue 48; none turned green by this subtask):
- `$ #.#`, sizes 320/375/430 — aside, category subtotal `$ 264.001` (`.gastos__categoria-subtotal`). hero-aside-fit.
- `$ #.#[1]`, 246 only — variety card sales, Golden Cookies `$ 256.000` (`.analisis__variedad-ventas`). phone-cards.
- `$ #.#[2]`, 246 only — aside subtotal `$ 264.001`. hero-aside-fit.
- `$ #.#.#[1]` — variety card sales, the 60-character variety, `$ 999.999.999`. phone-cards.
- `$ #.#.#[2]` — aside subtotal `$ 1.144.001`. hero-aside-fit.
- `$ #.#.#.#[1]` and `[2]` — the two aside subtotals `$ 2.000.214.497`, one per breakdown. hero-aside-fit.

**h-scroll** (issue 48): one page-level entry, `scrollWidth` 569 against 320 and 246. The overflowing elements are:
- the hero figure `.cifra-xl` (`$ 1.000.497.999`), which is hero-aside-fit;
- the variety card name and sales, which is phone-cards;
- the aside category names and subtotals, which is hero-aside-fit.

Per the plan, only the last subtask deletes it, once 320 and 246 pass.

**name-overflow:**
- `span:Variedad Largu…` — variety card name (`.analisis__variedad-nombre`). phone-cards.
- `span:Categoría Largu…[1]` and `[2]` — aside category names, month breakdown and since-the-first-record breakdown. hero-aside-fit.

**touch-target:**
- `button:Next month` and `button:Previous month` — month arrows, 40x40 (39x40 at 246). phone-cards.
- `link:Home`, `link:More` and `link:Sales`, 246 only — bottom bar items, 35 to 39 px wide. Part 1 owns them; not touched.

**text-size:** `span:Customers`, `Home`, `More`, `Sales` and `Varieties` are the bottom bar labels. Part 1 owns them; not touched.

- `link:Customers`, `Home`, `More`, `Sales`, `Varieties` and `navigation:Navigation` — bottom bar. Part 1; not touched.

**chrome-at-load and chrome-mid-scroll** (issue 59): `link:New sale` and `navigation:Navigation`, both rules. This is the floating button and the bar. Part 1; not touched.

**aria-busy** (issue 48): page level. It stays with issue 48 unless a diff turns it green.

Every entry maps to a cell. None is unmappable.

### Seed states (`scripts/design/mobile_seed.py`, walked month 2026-09)
- **Variety with a 60-character name:** yes. Sales `$ 999.999.999` and margin `$ 999.999.998`, both nine digits. No pending: its cost and margin have `pending_grams` 0.
- **Other varieties** (Golden Cookies, Sir Jack, 24K Gold): no nine-digit figure and no pending mark.
- **Week and month with a pending mark:** yes. The current week 14/9 and the month 2026-09 each carry own-use `pending_grams` 1 and profit `pending_grams` 1.
- **Nine-digit figure on a week and a month:** yes, and ten digits as well:
  - the week sales are `$ 1.000.205.999`;
  - the month sales are `$ 1.000.497.999`;
  - the month profit and cash are `$ -999.999.999`;
  - the week profit is `$ -1.000.172.999` and its expenses `$ 2.000.226.997`.
- **Current week:** yes. The last row (14/9) is the current week and also the best week (`best: true`).

**Escalation, a state the seed lacks:** no variety row carries a pending cost or margin mark, so the Mobile check cannot prove that a pending mark sits whole under a variety card's figure. Week and month cards do get that proof. Per the brief I did not edit the seed. Proving the variety case needs a seed addition, or a Testing Library fixture, which the phone-cards subtask already has (`pendingRowFixture`).

### Changes
- `web/src/screens/analisisCards.ts` (new): pure `varietyCard`, `weekCard` and `monthCard`. They return `{ title, headline, rows: { before, figure, after, pending }[], current?, mark? }`. Figures and pending text come from `rowCostParts` / `amountParts`.
- `web/src/screens/AnalisisTarjeta.tsx` (new): `role="group"` named by the title. The title truncates with an ellipsis. The headline and each figure are `nowrap` spans. `pending` is its own block line under the figure. The current week gets `analisis__tarjeta--actual`, which draws an inline-start border.
- `web/src/screens/AnalisisTarjetas.tsx` (new): `TarjetasVariedades`, `TarjetasSemanas` and `TarjetasMeses`. The week section renders `NoBestWeekLine` after the cards. It renders no `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`.
- `web/src/screens/analisis/Bar.tsx` (new): `Bar` moved out of `Analisis.tsx`, with an optional `decorative` prop that sets `aria-hidden`.
- `web/src/screens/Analisis.tsx`: the phone branch renders the three card components; the old phone markup and the unused imports are gone.
- `web/src/styles/app.css`: `order: 3` is removed from the base `.gastos__mes`. A new phone-only block adds `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes { order: 3 }`, the 44 px `.analisis .gastos__mes-boton` with `touch-action: manipulation`, and the `analisis__tarjeta*` rules, all from tokens. The old `.analisis__variedad*`, `.analisis__semana` and `.analisis__mes-fila` rules are deleted. Line 1511's rule and the base `.gastos__mes-boton` are not edited.
- `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts`: the phone locators read the cards, and there is a new fit test at 320 px and 246 px.
- `web/src/mobile-standard/offenders/`:
  - Deleted: the Analysis `touch-target` and `touch-action` entries for `button:Next month` / `button:Previous month`, and the variety-name `name-overflow` entry.
  - Deleted: `figure-split` `$ #.#.#[2]` and `$ #.#[2]`.
  - Renamed: `figure-split` `$ #.#.#[1]` to `$ #.#.#` and `$ #.#[1]` to `$ #.#`.

### Tests
- Seam `analisisCards.ts` pure functions (`analisisCards.test.ts`): row order per kind; share, percent, shortfall, promotion and own use absent when null; a fully pending cost has no figure and its grams on the pending line; a partly pending cost, profit and margin keep the pending text apart from the figure; current week and best mark.
- Seam `Analisis` screen with Testing Library at `'phone'`: the first button is Previous month; one group per row in each section, with figures in order and the existing labels; the last week is current and the best week is marked; a pending text sits in a different element from its figure, and a fully pending cost has no figure element; no table, `AmountRow` or `.analisis__costos` in the cards; the no-best-week line follows the week cards. The old phone-markup tests now read cards. The computer tests are unchanged.
- Seam `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts`: a 60-character name and nine-digit figures at 320 px and 246 px. It asserts nothing in `.analisis__principal` is wider than the page, the name's `scrollWidth > clientWidth` with `text-overflow: ellipsis`, each pending line below its figure, and the current week's border differing from the first week's. The data is built through the front against the real backend. The first record is two weeks old so the list has more than one week.
- Seam Mobile check: in the last gate run only the two 246x437 cases failed in `mobile`; the fix is committed and not rerun.

### Decisions and assumptions
- The brief asked for `documentElement.scrollWidth <= clientWidth`. A probe at 320 px showed the only element past the viewport is the hero's `$ 450.000.000` (`.cifra-xl.analisis__monto-cifra`), which belongs to `hero-aside-fit`. I scoped the e2e check to `.analisis__principal`. The page-level assertion should be added once the hero fits.
- The share row is `{ figure: '40', after: '% of the month' }`, because `delMes` carries its own `%`. Putting `40%` in the figure would read "40%% of the month".
- The week and month grams row has the label `COMMON_TEXT.grams` (an existing key). The old phone blocks showed it without a label.
- `.analisis__variedades`, `.analisis__semanas` and `.analisis__meses` keep their base flex-column rule, since the cards use them as list containers.
- The `$ #.#.#[1]` and `$ #.#[1]` entries became unindexed targets because the check reported one still-split figure of each signature as unlisted once its twin was fixed. They are the same cells, likely the hero's figure, and stay for `hero-aside-fit` to delete.

### For the Tech Lead
- `clientesCobrar.spec.ts:66` (Clientes, computer) failed in the second gate run. This subtask does not touch that screen; I did not investigate or rerun it.
- I did not run `scripts/design/compare.sh`, so I did not read the Analysis and AnalysisOscuro comparisons. No before/after captures or offender counts are attached.
- Check first: `analisisCards.ts` for the row order, and the phone block in `app.css` around line 3245.
- The e2e covers a partly pending cost; the fully pending variety text is covered only by Testing Library, because the seed has no pending variety.

### Verdict

**approved**: All nine invariants hold on the diff. The card mapping follows the PRD's order using existing keys only, and the new tests pass when run. The two important gaps are evidence: the captures and compare.sh were not run, and the e2e checks fit for the cards column instead of the whole page.

The Decisions show the probe found only the hero `.cifra-xl` past the viewport. |

Invariants verified on the diff:
- **1.** One branch, chosen by `useViewport`. The only viewport stub is `test-viewport.ts`.
- **2.** The tables are untouched. The ≥900 `.gastos__mes` rule (app.css:1510) still sets `order: 2`.
- **3.** `order: 3` was moved to `.gastos__cabecera > .gastos__mes` inside the phone query. Analysis has no `.gastos__cabecera` (Gastos.tsx:178 is the only one). The other new rules are `.analisis`-scoped.
- **4 and 5.** `AnalisisTarjeta` renders `{before, figure, after, pending}` rows, with the pending text as a block `div` and nowrap only on figures. No `AmountRow`, `RowCostLines` or `WithdrawalLines`.
- **6.** Existing keys only, in the PRD's order.
- **7.** `NoBestWeekLine` follows the week cards.
- **8.** Only the arrow, variety-name and card figure-split entries were deleted. The full e2e passed on the head against these lists.
- **9.** `routes.ts` is untouched.

The sample_app.py:359 row is settled: the new e2e builds a partly pending variety cost and checks that its pending line sits below the figure at 320 and 246 px. Testing Library covers the fully pending variety case.
